13a1(tsha): @#/drug cu bzhi//me rlung 'khor lo bzhu dang ba dan dbyings/dngos po gnas de ltar gsungs/de las bsdus pa 'bru brgyad spros/de las spos dang bsdus pa'i/lha rnams rdzogs pa gsang ba'i snying po don//dbang gis

17b4(tsha): bgyi/bdud rtsi mchog la bdag mnyan 'tshal/zhes gsol pas/bdud rtsi mchog gis bka' sprul pa/e ma'o las kyi sems dpa' che/phyi rab rnal 'byor don gyi phyir/bdud rtsi don zhus ya mtshan che'o//bdud rtsi don 'di ngas

17b6(tsha): pa de/srid gsum ban da bzang 'khor yug/ye nas ma bac+doMs rang bzhin sdod/de'i nang du skrag pa'i sman/bdud rtsi rtsa ba lo ma rnams/dbyer med dang du bsogs par byas/de la nges pa'i bsam pa yis/nges pa nyid du bsgrub par bya'o//

18a1(tsha): pa yangs skyon drug rnam par spangs pag//nya shu 'ang bcu ma yadan wa//rnams dang sdom pas bcings pa min//dang bar ra ba'i rnas brgyang la/pa ni rnams pa'i dus yin//rtags kyi don nyid mthong ba'am/
18a2(tsha): dusun dusu drug nyid la gdos yo byed ma lus ldan gyur na//'grub 'gyur 'di la the tshom med/mtshogs pa ni grub zag ring //de bas yo byad ldan par bya/bkra shes mtshan ldan ban nga lnga//ngam rdzas lnga dang sha lnga dang /phyi nang gas

18a4(tsha): bkod pa'i dkyil 'khor//bsgrub pa'i zhag drangs ji tsam par//bar chad bgegs rnams gsal ba'i phyir//phyi nang gsang ba'i rgyu 'dul bsdams//'bar ba 'tshams kyi dkyil 'khor//nyon mongs bag chags sbyangs pa'i phyir//'bum lnga'i

18b1(tsha): rnam bdud rtsi 'khyil ba'i dkyil 'khor//zla gam sgo shar ltas pa la//pad+ma 'dab ma brkyad bris la/rta mgrin dbang gi dkyil 'khor//lta ba bla med sdeng du blta//spyod pa bla med sdeng du spyad//dam tshigs bsrung ba bla med sdeng //phrin las bla med sde

18b4(tsha): dpag las 'das//zhes gsungaso//rta mchog rol pa'i tan+ta las//bdud rtsi chen po bsgrubs pa'i le'u ste rje gsum pa'o///de nas yang pad+ma las kyi sems dpa' nges//pad+ma gar gyis bdag po la/ma chags pa'i mchod

22b2(tsha): ting 'dzin gyi sbyang ba'o//g.yar ba dang ni spang ba'o//snang dang mi snang ngo bor rtag/mchod bstod sa rin gus par dbul//brtag pa de ru gnas par bya//ro lnga gsal dang yon tan stan//stug po dang ni yon tan skyon//srang slong gnyis
22b3(tsha): shes par bya//de ru dkyil 'khor ma gsol cig/mtha' dbus mnyam ste tshang ldan bya//las kyi phyag rgya gsal bar bya//las kyi phyag rgya gsal bar bya//chag chag rnam gsum rim par gdab//ba byung lnga'i byug pa yis//

22b5(tsha): rdzas ye shes lnga ldan pas//gsang mchog bsgrub phyir chag chag 'dab/armi maN+Da la/yis lha'i bzhugs gnas te//gnyis med mnyam pa'i dbyi su gdab par bya'o//zla gam dmar po'i gzhal yas su/gnyis med sbyor ba'i ngo//

22b7(tsha): byang chub sems kyi thig le 'debs/bo de rtsi ha ma 'dal la//mtshon gyi rnam pa lnga po yang //yi ge dpa' bo 'bru lnga yis//rin chen phye mar bslabs cing rigs kyi ngo bor gsal//de yis dkyil 'khor bri ba'o//dang po byi 'tshams chen po ste/
23a1(tsha): rgyal po bzhi la las bcol mchod bstong bya//bang kyis dbang ba las btsal bstod//'tsham su gnas pa'i las bya'o//de bas rdzas rnams dgram pa ste//byi nang gsang pa gsum du shes//dedas lha gang spyan drag
23a2(tsha): bstims//byin brlabs bkod cing skan bsdu na gsol//brtan par bzhugs shing 'dod yon gsol//dang po snyen pa dang ni nye bsnyen bya//de nas bsgrub pa dang ni bsgrub chen po//de ltar las kyi rnar bstan pa'o//rta mchog rol pa'i tan+t+ra

23a5(tsha): stsal ba/yi ge 'phreng ba 'phro 'du ste//yi ge de'i 'phro 'du las//sngags kyi phreng ba 'bru brgyad gsal//dang po 'phro 'dra lha rnams bskul//gnyis pa ma mo mkha'agro bskul//gsum pa las kyi mkha'agro bsnyen//zhi ba gnyis med ngang du
23a6(tsha): bsnyen/'phro 'du 'phro ba ma dang bsngal//de las phar mchod tshur dngos grub//tsha tsha'i tshul du 'phro 'du bya//de ni sgrub pa chen po zhes/phrin las bsgrub pa zhes bya ste//gnas pa mnyen po bsten ni bsnyen pa ste//'phro 'du bsgrub pa chen por

23b1(tsha): gtso//gnan pa'i las rnams bshad du gsol/ces gsol to//dbang gis rgyal pos bka' stsal pa/nyon cig las kyi sems dpa' nyon//gnan pa'i las rnams ngas bshad kyis/kho nar nges par yid la bzung //ma dang chen por sprul
23b2(tsha): bA la/ga go za bod gnan pa ni//las mthar phyir gnan pa'o//de ni gnan pa'i las dag ni//'khor lo rtsibs ni brgyad pa bri//dbus su yan lag nying lag dang //mgo dang bcas pa'i gdong ba de/nyon mongs lnga dang ldan par bri//tha mar 'dzam bu grI
23b3(tsha): ga~M dbyibs//rgya gram rtse la 'bar ba'o//bkra mi shis pa'i zag pas bcing //gdug pa'i mtshon cha dang bcas la//sha khrag rdzas dang bcas par bshams//mon nag rabs chang thod par gzhug/rgya mtsho ngos dang shing gcig gi//de ni mnan pa'i
23b4(tsha): lasu shes//de ltar mnan na ldang mi 'gyur//zhes gsungaso//rta mchog rol pa'i tan+ta las/gnan pa'i le'u ste so bzhi pa'o////de nas pad+ma las kyi sems dpas//bcom ldan 'das pad+ma dbang gi rgyal po la 'di skad ces gsol to//

23b6(tsha): cig ces gsol to//dbang gi rgyal pos bka' stsal ba/las kyi sems dpa' zhu ba leg so//the tshom thad gcad par bya'o//'khor ba dag ni 'dul ba'i tshe/brtul zhugs spyod pa ji ltar spyad//lta ba lte ba 'di ltar blta//bdag nyid ye shes
23b7(tsha): dri bor bstan//'khor ba sgral ba'i zhing du blta//dam tshigs bsgral ba gsungs pa'o//dam tshigs sbal ba chen po'o//thogs rdug med pa'i brtul zhugs kyis//the tsom med pa'i sgral ba de//brtul zhugs chen po 'di ltar spyad //smyon pa'i chas
24a1(tsha): @#su zhugs nas ni//las dang ma lus skrod kyang bgrod//drag po'i las la smang mi bya//lam mi gzung ba phyag mi btsal//'phreng ba 'khar la de bzhin gzung //'khros pa'i cha lugs chen po yis//'gro 'dug ste su
24a2(tsha): /byas nas ni//phyogs bzhir gdug pa ring ro gdon//rta'i rtul shugs chen po ni//brjid pa'i tshul du zas nas ni//mdu gsum 'chang cing nga ro gdon//lags pa 'dun dang rkang pa rgyab du gdengs//ngal dub med par mdun gam phyag//hriHlas
24a3(tsha): rta mchog 'bar ba'i tshogs/khro bo sdeng gdug pa'i nga ro gdengs//rta mgo can gyi khams sum bkang //'phro 'dul gdug pa ma lus 'dul//rta mchog can yang chur du 'bebs//gdug pa'i tshogs la brjod cing rgyug/'di ni
24a4(tsha): rta'i brtul zhugs yin//khyi'i rtul shugs drag 'bar ste//brjid par gdug pa'i nga ro can//g.yas pa lag gi 'du byed ste//g.yon pa brgyab brkyang 'chang ba'i tshul//dza yis yis 'bar ba'i khyi//khro bo gdug pa'i nga ro gding //khyi mgo can gyis
24a5(tsha): khams su gang //gdug pa'i nga ro phyogs bcur sgrogs//phyogs skyong rgyal rnams 'dar khrag kyag/ngal dub med pa'i ting 'dzin gyis//gdugs pa ma lus 'joms pa'o//bya'i brtul shugs chen po yis//drag po brjid ldan pa yis//srid
24a6(tsha): pa'i gdugs pa sprul ba de//sku dang ye las bye mgo'i tshogs//'bar la 'phros pas khams gsum spros bkang ste//g.yas g.yon thabs dang shes rab bshog pa gdengs//sngon du rdo rje sder mos bcum//lding zhing rol pas rgyu drug
24a7(tsha): sgrol la mkhas//gdug pa ma lus sgral ba'o//phag gi brtul shugs chen po ni//gti mug 'joms ni phag rgod gdug pa'i sde//swa ta las ni phag gi tsho gas//khams suM ma lus bkang bya ste//tha~Mng 'joms shing thams cad za//gdug pa'i
24a8(tsha): kha mngar med pa'i zasu bza'//gang dang phred pa yang ni mnan cing brdeg par bya//khang yang ngal dub med par rgyug/'khros pa'i tshul gyi las rnams bya//brtul zhugs bzhi'i cha su zhugs nas ni//
24b1(tsha): nga ro yongs gding //sna la rta'i 'phro 'dug 'khrungs//lag pa phag gi chags par ldan//phaT kyis lcags ni rab tu 'phro//hUM gis 'phro 'du stangs thabs byed//dub dang ngal med rgyug la dga'//khams suM ma lus 'joms la dga'//m+b+hi sprul
24b2(tsha): pa'i khro bo sprin dpung bzhin du spros//dpa' bo ting 'dzin ldan pa yis//thaMd 'joms shing gdugs pa'i klu'i tshogs//dpa' dang ldan pa'i ting 'dzin bye bo zhes gsungs so//rta mchog rol pa'i tan+t+ra las/brtul zhugs bzhi dka' thub smin

24b4(tsha): ni rnam pa du ma zhig yod//gar gyis dbang gi bsha gsol//zhes gsol to//dbang gi rgyal pos bka' stsal pa/pad+ma las kyi sems dpa' nyon cig/legs par nyon la yid la zung /ma dang sems can chen po la/rtsa ni nyi khri chig stong ste/

24b6(tsha): gsum ni srog shing ste//phung po lnga ni 'khor lo rtsegs pa bzhin/sa bon yi ge rang bzhin//de la yan lag bzhi gyis ste//sgo bzhi drag po'i 'jug so//khug bzhi khug gnyis sdug pa ste//gnas gzhir gzir cing sgo bzhir dpung //rgyun lam

25a1(tsha): #ring tu tshangs pa lha'i rigs yin te//drags po myur ba srin po khro bo ste//gdug pa 'dul ba dbang phyug tshangs pa ste//gnyis med rtag par rga ba dreg pa can gyi rigs//de la dregs pa rnams pa dgu//zhe sdang rang bzhin/

25b1(tsha): tshigs smad du 'byung ba'o//dkyil 'khor sems kyi yul ste//dkyil 'khor slad du 'byung ba'o//ci yang ci yang byang chub sems te/dkyil 'khor sma du byung ba'o//ji ltar snang ba dang ni mi snang ba dang ni/mi snang ba gnyis dang gnyis med bcas pa semasu blta ste/

26a1(tsha): lnga'i bshags pas sdig pa 'tshams med byang bar 'gyur//lha rnams kyi dam tshig ni mtshon rnon po ltar bsrung bar bya'o//slob dpon mig sgron ma lta bur bsrung //mchod pas bsrung dang blta bar bya zhes gsung so//

26a4(tsha): sa'i cho ga bya ba de mgom yeng dang gang thar bya'o//rta gon la ni gnas par bya'o//bum pa sta gon du gnas pa dang //lha lta gon du gnas pa'o//dkyil 'khor dbus su bla ga la//sgo cig par ni dgod par bya'o//tha ma zla gam brgyad kyis bskor//sgo dang

26a6(tsha): gzung thag gtsang ma'i gzugs kyis gdags//dkyil 'khor dang ni bum pa lta gon la gnas pa'o//de nas slob ma lha gon la gnas par bya'o//lus gtsang gos brgyan ku sha'i bdan//der ni sems skyed zlas pa bya//dang po dbang gis rdzas rnams sog//
26a7(tsha): slob dpon dang ldan pa des//slob ma la ni bskur bar bya'o//skyed dang spyan drang bzhugs gsol mchod//stong nge zhu ba gsol ba'o//rdzas kyi dbang dang lta ba'i dbang //gsang ba'i dbang dang bcas par bskur//dang por dmigs ni bye bar bya//sngon gyis
26b1(tsha): /dmigs mkhan rgyal po yis//shel gyi btsags bu thogs nas ni//ma rig rab rigs gsal ba dang /bu'i rab rig gsal bar bya'o//tsag Shu pra be sha ma 'dal la hUM/e ma rang 'byung bcom ldan dang gi rgyal po sku 'khor ba yongasu sgyung ba'i phyir//

26b4(tsha): la mkhas pa dang /zhing chen g.yang gzhi thabs la mkhas pa dang //stag lpags mi zad dpa' bar ldan pa dang //na bza' gsum dang brgyan gyis dbang cho ga thob par shog/bse khrab smug po la na bza' chas rnams kyis//ma lus mnga' snyems

27a1(tsha): ye shes gnyis med dbang thob shog/rdo dbang gis phur ba 'di byin pas//yes dbyis la bur ba 'debs par sheg/bza' dang bca' dang btung ba bgo/ mchog phun sum tshogs pa'i//bkra shis dam pa thos

27b4(tsha): byang chub sems//thaMd ma lus byang chub sems//sems las ma byung gang yang med//yod pa sems la med pa sems//gnyis ka yang ni sems nyid de//thams cad byang chub sems gsal//de ni lta ba byang chub sems/zhes gsungs//

27b7(tsha): gar gyi dbang phyug yab yum la//gsang ba'o ma bzhi skor nasu//sgo la 'khor lo rkang gcig bzhi//de nas su ni bkod na su//rtsa spro rang re sngags kyi bya//oM dang ru lu ma ma ma las skyed//sha khrag dmar dang lnga lding gur//bsten pa'i rdzas dang mchod
28a1(tsha): pa'i rdzas//brdeg pa'i rdzas dang rnam pa gsum mo//bstan pa'i sha sna khrag sna dmar la bstan//bsgrub pa 'brub khung ling ga ste//gdug pa'i rdzas kyis brdeg pa'o//ma bzhi yan chad rdzas bkram nas//tha ma
28a2(tsha): gnan sreg 'phang ba'o//de yis las kyis the tsom med//rta mchog rol pa'i tan+d+ha las//dbang gis ma ba'i bsgrub pa'i le'u ste/zhes gnyis pa'o///de nas las kyi sems dpa' gsol ba gsan nas kyang //bsgrub pa las kyi rim
28a3(tsha): bshadata/dang po sngon du 'gro ba bya//de nas dkyil 'khor bri bar bya//zla gam zlum po pad+ma bzhi yis bskor//khyams dang sgo rnams de bzhin bya//de la dam rdzas dpar po bsog//dang po ting 'dzin rnam gsuM ste//gzhal yas khang de skyed pa de//'thub
28a4(tsha): ba rims dang brum dang ba//skad cig sngags brjod rdzogs tshul bskyed//de la bskyed nas spyan drangs bzhagasu gsol//phyag rgyas sngags kyis bya'o//de la bdud rtsi gtor mas rak+tas mchod //dngos grub rnam gsum tshul bzhin bslang //mchod
28a5(tsha): mchod cing bstod pa'o//dbang gi lha ni dbus su ste//pad+ma drug la khams brgyad bkod//sgo ru sgo ma de bzhin//de nas de la las kyang bcol//smon lam btab cing snyen bsgrub bya//lha rgod pa yang rims bzhin bya//sdong tshig ma'i khrag gis ni//
28a6(tsha): dur khrod ras la ling ga bri//'brub khung nang du de bcug la//de la dgug zhugs stim gdab ste//bsgral nas lhag ma bstabs pa yis//rjes shul dag la bro brdung //bdug pa'i rdzas rnams ma lus 'dzeg/mkhar nag skye nas gdags
28a7(tsha): par bya'o//de yis dbang drag las bya'o//ces gsung so//rta mchog rol pa'i tan+ta las//dbang gi rgyal po kham brgya ma drug gis bskor ba'i le'u ste//zhes gsum pa'o////de nas yang pad+ma las kyi sems dpa' yis//dbang gi/

28b2(tsha): zlum po gru bzhi dang /phyi rim 'khor lho bskor de//sgo dang sgo khyud de bzhin byed//dbusu dbang gi rgyal po de//zlum bu dang ni go la ma//'khor lo rtsibs la sbyang brgyad de//rtsibs 'chan la ni khyi brgya do//gru bzhi la ni long ba'i tshogs//
28b3(tsha): /sgo bzhi dag la khro bo'o//gzhan yang rdzas kyis rim pa dgod//dbusu khang bu kha sbyar te//de ru dam rdzas bsag pa'o//phyi ma rang re'i rdzas dgram ste//rten rdzas sgrub rdzas brdeg pa'i rdzas//sha sna khrag sna 'bru rnams ni//bstan pa'i rdzasu bstan
28b4(tsha): pa'o//'bru bcud sman dang bcas pa ni//de snying sna dang bcas pa kun//de ni bsgrub pa'i rje su bya//gu gul nyung nag rdo phye dug/de ni brdeg pa'i rdzas yin no//gzhan yang rkang rdzas la sogs bshams//ting 'dzin dang ni dbang mchog rim//gzhan yang /
28b5(tsha): lha skyed spyan drangs bzhugs gsol phyag 'tshal mchod//smon lam dag pa de 'dod gsol//de nas bsnyen bsgrub lesu bya//de nas ling ga bcas la rdzas drag bsags//de nas btab dang bsgrub pa dang //gdab dang drag po'i las bya'o//de yis
28b6(tsha): gang la dmigs pa bsnabs ces gsungaso//rta mchog rol pa'i tAn+ta las//dbang gi rgyal po la spyang brgyad khyi brgyad dang go la ma brgyad dang khyi spyang long ba bsgrub pa'i le'u ste zhe bzhi pa'o///de nas yang las kyi
28b7(tsha): sems dpas//bcom ldan 'das la 'di skad ces gsol to//e maM'o dbang gi rgyal po che//stobs dang ting 'dzin ldan pa des//las kyi bsgo mo bsgrub pa sha gsol//dbang gi rgyal pos bka' stsal pa//stobs ldan ting 'dzin chen po dri
29a1(tsha): dang po las kyi rim pa bya//de nas gzugs rnyen dkyil 'khor bri//dbus ni zla gam sgo gcig pa//ri mig brgyad du bris nasu//mtha' ma pad+ma gru bzhi sgo dang bcas//dbus su dbang gi bdag po de//ri mig rnams la
29a2(tsha): ke ri brgyad//pad+ma dbang phyug sing ngam//nyi shu rtsa brgyad sadri bgod//sgo la khro ba'i de bzhin bgod//ting 'dzin kyang de bzhin reg/gnyis med pas snyim pa'o//tang 'dzin rnam gsum rim bzhin//hUM las gzhal yas 'byung bas chags

29a4(tsha): pe ta li ha/pu ka si ha/kas ma ri ha/sman sha ni ha/tsan da li ha/sing nga mu kha he/bya kri mu ga he/sho na mu ka he/kha kha mu ka he/kri ta mu ka he/hu lu kha mu ka/ma nu rag+ma sri/pra ma ni/re'u dwi/be sna gaM/ko ma ri/in+d+ra ya/badz+ra bing ga la ma/
29a5(tsha): a mi tam/so'u mI/da na dam/rag sha si/da na dam/tu tri ra ma tram e ka ra tsa raM ma nu ha ram/si ti kaM/ba ya be/ma ha ra ma na dz+ra/ag ni ye/ba ra hi/tsa mun ti/pu ta na/ba ru ni/badz+ra ma hA tsaM ka la/tsa ka la/badz+ra/sum/
29a6(tsha): pa ka ra na/badz+ra lam po da ra ya/de ltar skyed mchod las bya'o//drag po rdzas dang ling ga bca' rdzogs par bya//tha ma mnan sreg 'phang dang sbyar//ces gsung so//rta mchog rol pa'i tan+t+ra las/dbang gi rgyal po la ki ri dang sing

29b2(tsha): yis bcas//yi ge hUM gis sgra brjod pas//che ba'i yon tan dpag las 'das/de las dbang gi sku g.yas te//dbu ni nyi shu rtsa gcig ste/nyi shu las ni yis ngo bor gsal//phyag ni brgyad cu rtsa gnyis byung //zhabs ni dmar po bcu
29b3(tsha): ru shar tan bza' gdugs pa'i spags pa sna tshogs dang //bsre khrab la sogs gsol ba'o//log lha gdug byir brgyan dang dbu//ma lus tshang bar bsnams pa'o//yum ni thugs kar sbas pa'o//sangs rgyas stong gi 'thung gnaso//

29b7(tsha): gi rgyal po rdzas yin//thod pa 'tshal ba bcu drug tu/khrag zhal nyung dug bcas pa bgang \u0f7fyi ge 'bru brgyad bkod pa ni//ki ri sing nga'i rdzas yi no//nag po dum med thod pa ru/snying sna tshogs dang sha khrag dang //dug dang nyungs mnga'/

33a4(tsha): dang /bsgrub pa dang ni sgrub che no//bsnyen bsgrub bzhi ru shes par bya//dbang gi rgyal pos yab yum par//ma las thog gi rgyal mo bzhi ru byung //zhabs kyi gza' chen po ra hu la//ya ma nag po bton te//de ltar bkod de gnas pa las//dbu suM gtso bo/
33a5(tsha): hri la so//dkyil 'khor de la rim paro//oM hr-i ru lu sa ma ya dza//yau ma snying la ma khrag du ga pi sa mA ya dza//ma ma rag sha trag tu tsar li ma sham pa de lam b+h+yo//rak+Sha kha la ti/sa ma ya b+h+yo//ga pi sa ma ya ru ru tsa ba tsa ba drag tu tsar b+h+yo//chang pa nag po'i
33a6(tsha): bong pa ru 0/rnam la skya rengs gzer chen te//bar du lha gnyen gdug ser rta//sa la gdug pa'i khrag zer ro//de'i las rnam grub pa'o//rta mchog rol pa'i tan+ta las//thog 'bebs pa dngango ser ba 'bebs pa'i le'u steng nga bzhi pa'o//
33a7(tsha): de nas ling ga drag po bcas nas ni//mtshan dang ldan pa'i ling ga la//seng ldeng phur pas sdig cing gzir//khrag sna 'dus pa sme bar bcas//phod pa dag du blugs nas ni//dur khrod rkang gis myu gus bya//'o ma sna gsum bsres nas ni//gdugs
33a8(tsha): ma las kyim pa gong bzhin te/gdug pa'i ga zug ma lus dang /snying sna dang ni sman gyis mchod//ga du rog sha la shaM ta pob//drag leng bab ling //sa ma ru ti la b+he//dug dang ma chis sbyar bar bya'o//gnas gsuM dag du cu ru gzhug/
33b1(tsha): tha'i shing la me sbyar la//dug sngags bzlas shing thab bcas la//thal 'jam dur khrod sol bas phyogs bzhir gtor//de'i sa thob ser 'bebs par byed//rta mchog rol pa'i tan+ta las/rdo thog ser 'bebs pa'i le'u ste nga lnga pa'o///de nas
33b2(tsha): yang las kyis songs/dbang gi rgyal po la 'di skad ces gsol to//bcom ldan gsang ba dbang gi bdag/phyi rabs don du yang dang yang //zor gyi las rnams bsha gsol//ces gsol to//bcom ldan dbang gis bka' stsal pa//pad+ma las kyi seMnga'
33b3(tsha): nyon/dbang gi zor du bstan pa ni//bsgrub pa chen po rdzogs pa'i tshe//drag po gzor du bya ba ni//dbang gi rgyal po skyes bu yis//gshed ma dred pa'i ban d+ha ru/khyod gu shi ba'i dri ma can//de lta bu'i thod pa ru//drag po'i rdzas rnams ma lus bso//gdug
33b4(tsha): pa'i rdzas kyi de bkang la//byin gyis brlabs cing bskul bya ste//gang dmigs gnasu 'phong byas la//de la zor gyis gdug bdag bskul//yi ge bzhi'i sa 'phro 'du bya//zhe sdang gdug pa'i ting 'dzin bsgom//zor gyis las mkhan ma tshogs bskul/zhe sdang
33b5(tsha): ldan pas brdeg par bya'o//ces gsung so//rta mchog rol pa'i tAn+da las/dbang gi gzor gyi le'u ste drug pa'o///de nas yang pad+ma las kyi seMngs//dbang gi rgyal po la gsol pa/kye ma dbang gi rgyal po dag/phyi rabs rnams
33b6(tsha): kyi don du ni/rgyun tu ting 'dzin ji ltar lags//dpa' bo dbang gi rgyal pos bka' stsal pa//las kyi so 'a nyon cig/phyi rabs kyi don du rgyan gyis ting 'dzin bstan par bya'o//mdun du bzhag ste sems bskyed de//ting 'dzin gsum dang ldan par bya//snod
33b7(tsha): ni dag pa gzhil yas la//bcud ni dag pa lha'i tshogs//gnyisu med pas lha mos mchod//spyan drangs bzhugs gsol phyag btsal nas//mchod pa'i 'dod don gsol ba gang ba//dgyes bskyed de gshegasu gsol//oM badz+ra ba len ta bi sha tsa tra ma/
34a1(tsha): @b+h+rUM oM d+hI shang ya ma ya sa ma ya phaT/byin rlabs mchod lha rnams dgyes pa ste//bstod cing bskul la phrin las bcol//bca' ba thaMd mchod pa de/btung ba zad bdud rtsir mchod//lha mchod bdag rol dngos grub yin//
34a2(tsha): rna mchog rol pa'i tAn+ta las//rgyun gyi ting 'dzin g+hi le'u steng bdun pa'o///pad+ma las kyi sems dpa' nyon//l-i ge 'phreng ba'i bzlas pa dang //dgongs pa'i phrin las chen po ni//dang po phyi mo 'khor lor gsal//bde ba chen po gas
34a3(tsha): yas sbyin te 'khor lo sum cu rtsa gnyis//phyi mo yi ge oM du gsal//de las 'bru brgyad hU lu tshogs//yig 'bru rin chen phreng ba ste//yi ge 'od kyi 'phreng bar gnas//longs spyod 'khor lo 'dab bcu drug/phyi mo yi ge aM dmar po//de la hu lu 'bru brgyad
34a4(tsha): bskor//thig le lta bur bskor ba'o//chos kyi 'khor lo 'dab ma brgyad//phyi mo yi ge hUM du gnas//tha ma hu lu 'bru brgyad bskor//rin chen tsar la rgyus pa bzhin/til 'bru bzhin du gnas pa'o//lte ba rin chen gzhal yas te//'khor lo drug cu gnyis
34a5(tsha): dang ldan//chen mo yi ge sA ser po//de la hu lu 'bru brgyad bskor//tsha tsha lta bur gnas pa'o//las kyi gzhal yas 'bar ba bzhi//me dang rlung gi 'khor lo gnas//yi ge zhu dang gru gsum ba ngan mtshan//phyi mo yi ge ha ljang ku//de la hu lu 'bru brgyad bskor
34a6(tsha): tsha tsha bzhin du gnas pa'o//de las 'phro dang 'du ba ste//phrin lngas ma lus rdzogs pa'o//zhes gsung so//rta mchog rol pa'i tan+t+ra las//yi ge 'phreng ba bstan pa'i le'u ste nga brgyad pa'ong dgu pa'o///de nas yang bang gi sems dpa' la/
34a7(tsha): dbang gis rgyal pos bka' stsal pa//nyon cig pad+ma las kyi sems dpa' che//ngas gsungs pa la the tshom med//nga yi gsungs pa'i rgyud 'di la/yon tan dang gi mi lang ste//ji ltar snang ba yang nga'i spyod yulo//chos 'di thong nga nyid do//sku rang nyid//gsung
34b1(tsha): dacing //thugs pa cid do//yon tan pastod//phrin las pa nyid do//ning khams pad kyang banyid do//rgyud 'di la ni spyod pa dag/'ad med bde ldan dbyings nyid//ces gsung so//rta mchog rol pa'i tan+d+ha las/che ba dang yon tan btan pa'i le'u /
34b2(tsha): drug cu pa'o///sang+yas ston tshe la bstod//zad kun gyi re bskong ba//yid bzhin nor bu lta bu ste//dngos 'dod bskong ba khyod la bstod//log tog 'khor ba mya lus pa//bdud rtsi lta bur mnyam sbyar nas//ma lus bde ba che 'dod
34b3(tsha): pad+ma bdud rtsi chen po khyod la bstod//'khor ba mun pa lte bu la//gsal la dri ma med pa'i//sgron me lte bur sku mnga' ba//sangs rgyas ston pa khyod la bstod//ces bstod ngo //rta mchog rol pa'i tan+ta las/'khor gyis bstod pa'i le'u ster
34b4(tsha): gcig pa'o///de nas dbang gis rgyal pos//'khor rnams la//thad ma lus dbang gi rgyal//dbang rgyal dam pa 'di stan te//sems nyid skye ba med pa la//skye mched dang las zing 'byung //'byung ba nyid ni dngos po med//
34b5(tsha): dngos med dang la de bzhin gnas//gnas pa nyid kyang bcom pa'o//ma bcos dang las rang bzhin gnas//sangs rgyas rnams kyis dgongs pa'o//ces gsung so//rta mchog rol pa'i tan+t+ra las//dbang gi rgyal po pad+ma las kyi senga' la tshig drug lta
34b6(tsha): bsgom nges pa'i don bstan pa'i le'u ste re gnyis pa'o///de nas dbang gi rgyal pos/pad+ma las kyi seda' la sogs pa'i/'khor rnams la bka' stsal pa//pad+ma las kyi sems dpa' la sogs pa'i//'khor rnams
34b7(tsha): kyis nga'i rgyud 'di tshangs shig/lhogs shig/rgyal gyi rtse mo la thogs shig//kun chub par bgyis cig/dar zhing rgyas par gyis shig/bsti btang gnasu gyis shig/bstad do thub par gyis shig/ces bsgo ba bta //rta mchog rol pa'i tan+da las/
35a1(tsha): @rgyud yongs bzung zhing gtad pa'i le'u ste re gsum pa'o///rnamatshi garol pa'i rgyad chen po rdzog so///de nas yang pad+ma las kyi sems dpa' des/bcom ldan 'das dpal rang gi
35a2(tsha): rgyal po 'di skad ces gsol to//kye ma bcom ldan dbang gis bdag/'gro ba dag gi don sladadu//dbang rgyal las ni bshad du gsol//ces gsol to//dbang gi rgyal pos bka' stsal pa/pad+ma las kyi seMde'i che//'gro ba dag gi don slad/
35a3(tsha): dbang gi las bsdus thang bshad par bya'o//dbang chen khyad par bstan pa ni//dbu'i dkyil 'khor chas kyis bgyis pa dang //zhabs kyi gdan khri gdul bya spungs pa ni//thaMd lus pa med par mnyam rdzogs chen por snang //rang gzhan lus pa
35a4(tsha): med pas bdag nyid chen por spang //oM pad+ma shri he ru ka/a kar sha ya/ban da ya/ban da ya/to sha ya/to sha ya/hUM hA/sa ma ya ra ga ti hUM hrI hri/stong nyid mkha' ltar dag pa'i dbyings rum nas//ting 'dzin rol pa'i rdzu 'phrul sna tshogs/
35a5(tsha): sku gsung thugs kyi bdag nyid hUM gi dang /rgyal ba rgya mtsho kun gyi dngos grub ni//'phro 'du na mkhar 'khrug pa'i byin brlabs las//dpal gyi thugs gcig nges par 'byung ba ni//sgyu ma'i rang bzhin yis rgya mtsho ste/dbang gi rang bzhin thabra/
35a6(tsha): kyi phyag rgyar gsal//kha dog me 'bar byu ru'i 'od 'khrigs pas//gsum zil gyis gnon cing khamasu sbyin sreg mdzad//ral pa dmar gang 'jig rten mtha' las 'das//ska tsher 'bar bas zhe sdang gdug pa'i tshogs/khro bo dbang bsdud
35a7(tsha): 'jigs pa'i hUM sgra can//spyan mig me 'bar stong gsum nyi zla 'khrugs//smin ma klog 'gyu dug gsum rtsad nas gcod//snyan gyi 'od 'phro 'brugi rdo khong song nas//'brug gi sgra sgrogs khro bo'i hUMsma can//shangs kyi glong rum khro bo'i
35b1(tsha): dkyil 'khor nas/mtshon gyi char dang bskal pa'i bu yug 'bebs//haM lnga 'brel ba'i mkha' dbyings klong rus nas rgyad lnga sgrol mdzad gangs ri 'bum gyis gtams//leb rgan me 'bar pad+ma stong gi ljags//ut+pal dmar po 'ong
35b2(tsha): zer klog du 'gyu//ag tshom gser 'bar sma ra dmar pos ni//log rtog bdud kyi 'jig rten thal bar rlog/phyag gi khyad par shin tu mi bzad te//ye shes bcu drug 'khrug pa'i dkyil 'khor nas//gdul bya thabs la mkhas pa
35b3(tsha): 'phro men tshogs//phyag dang phyag rgyar thabs kyis 'brel pa ni//zhi gnas lhag mthong gzung du gsal ba bzhin//byams dang snying rje phyogs ris mi mnga' yang //dur khrod 'dul ba'i don la thod rus rak+tas 'gengs//srin po 'dul slad
35b4(tsha): lcags kyu zhags par bcas//klu chen 'dul ba'i don la sdig 'jug bskal pa rang bar//bdud bzhi 'dul ba'i don la rdo 'bar brdeg/khro bo zil gyis gnon pa'i don la sgyu snying chang par 'dzums//zhabs kyi khyad par shin tu mi zad//khro brgyad
35b5(tsha): gan srub sre zhing srid gsum zhabs kyis gnon//gdugs mdzad gdugs 'joms lha la phyag 'tshalo//chos nyid dbyings kyi yis zab mo ni//kun tu bzang mo yum gyi b+hi ga la//sku gsung thugs kyi yon tan rigs pa'i 'phrul//dbu lnga
35b6(tsha): smad du byung bas yis lnga'i ngang //phrin las sma du byung bas zhabs kyi che ba brgyad//'khor tshogs lhun gyis grub pa'i phyag mtshan bcu drug tshogs//mkha' dbyings yongasu khyab pa'i rin chen bshog pa las//nyi zla bskas pas rgyal ba stong
35b7(tsha): phrag tshogs/ rin chen rgyal gyis phul du byung bas ni//srid gsum 'khor ba'i bag chags thal bar klog/rdo rje ze'u phran rig pa'i 'phro 'du las//sbrul pa rdul phran khams gsum khyab par 'khrugs//rdu gu dpung brgyan klu rgyal 'bum gyis
36a1(tsha): la zlas dz+nyA byas sa mas rnams gas la//de ba 'das stsa bas nga //rnams bzug smras napa'amam pa klags pas/ rdzu ga rgyas pa rgyam pas dang du wA /khang
36a2(tsha): dngas tsas ngam brjid mi chung ste//sgra chen dmar ba ram par sbal ba bzhin/ldang yir 'gyel ba'i 'ong bar gsang ltar 'gyu/na bza' brgyan gyis cha lugs mi 'ang de//nang lpags 'bum gyis srungs pa'i srungs lam

36a4(tsha): bya'i thugs kha nas//gsing chen snyen khungs rgyad pa'i btsun mo ni//khro mo gzi ldan rgyal ba kun gyi yum//'das dang ma byon kun gyi 'byung gnas ma//gzi byin tshe mchog gnasu bstim pa dang //rgya drug rnam shes dbyingasu bsdus la
36a5(tsha): mkhas//thugs dam gser 'phreng yab yum ye nas 'brel//dgyes pa'i longs spyod don dam dbyings na che/dga' byed dregs pas myos pa'i lha tshogs mang po kun//dbang drags 'joms pa'i zhabs kyi snol mar gzigs/
36a6(tsha): tshogs chen mdun par bsdud cing nye zla sku la brgyan//dug gsum rtsad nas gcod pas haM lnga zhal nas 'baraka/rta skad gtsug na sgrogs shing 'od zer char du 'bebs//tshogs chen rdzogs pa nyid la bstod//rta mchog ril pa'i tan+t+ra las/

36b2(tsha): sems ma brten ma rin chen 'bar//chos dang las dang bsdud ma skyod ma'o//lho ru gzigs pa'i dbu'i gzhal yas na//ldan sbyang rin chen phreng ba gzi brjid thogs//rgyal mtshan zhags pa gzhad pa bde ba'o//nub du gzigs pa'i dbu'i
36b3(tsha): gzhal yas na//dbyangs dkar chos dang len blon po me//'khor lo ljags sgrogs smra ba bde ba'o//byang du gzigs pa'i dbu'i gzhal yas na//'khor lo lcags sgrog sgrol las dang gar byed bsrung ba dbyug//gnod sbyin dril 'khrol
36b4(tsha): 'bebs ma'o//skyong bzangs sems pa sgeg mo rgyal pos gdugs//'dod pa lcags kyi legs par sgrol ma'o//sngags dang phyag rgya ting 'dzin bsgyur ba'o//zhes gsungs so//rta mchog rol pa'i tan+t+ra las/phyag rgya bstan pa'i les

38a5(tsha): ya ma na/ kha la ru la shaM rbad/ma tang shau b+h+yo//ma mai ting laM ting lo rbad//ru ru shaM rbad/srog rtsa paM la rag sho ni rbang /ma ma ru ti shaM laM shar yaM//ba ta kha le ya ma rbadaka ka la ma du ru rbad/ka/be ba pe na sha mthang //khri mung ban rbad//shag ti rbad/sma ra
38a6(tsha): rbad ta ru ru ro shan rbad b+h+yo//de ltar bsnyen po sgrub mchod dang las bcol gtad//de yis gang 'dod las rnams 'grub ces gsungaso//rta mchog rol pa'i tan+t+ra las/rtsa khro ma mo bcu gsu bsgrub pa'i le'u ste re dgu pa'o//

38b1(tsha): rgyal pos bka' stsal pa/pad+ma las kyi sems dpa' nyon cig/las rnams grub par byed pa'i srid pa'i rgyal mo zhes bya ba//rgyal po dzaM dur zhes bya ste/rgyal mo zhes pa kun gyis rje/dza zhes bya ba 'grugs pa ste//thaMd bsod cing bsdud pas dur zhes bya/
38b2(tsha): rgyal mo chen mo bsgyangs pa na/skyed mos tshal lam chu gling ngam//yang na 'bru shing 'tsham par bsgrub//de ru dkyil 'khor gru bzhi sgo dang bcas//dbus su zla gam phyi ni pad+ma brgyad//de ru mtshan ldan rje rigs thaMd pa ru ka 'byung ba'i bcud dang rtsi thog
38b3(tsha): 'bras rnams dang //sha khrag la sogs bsog nas ni//de la bsgrub cing las bcol bya//ha hi srid pa'i rgyal mo khyod kyis//rang gis gsang ba'i sngags rnams spros cig ces pa bsgo'o//rgyal mo chen mos yang smras pa//hi hi mngon rdzogs nga
38b4(tsha): gi rgyal//srog tsa gsang ba 'di 'bul zhes//byin gyis rlob la dbang bzhi bskur//dbang gi rgyal pos ji skad sgo ba bzhin du bgyi'o//smod pa'i gsang sngags 'di rnams lags//oM e ka dza ra dza du sa ma ya ru ru sha~M b+h+yo//sa ma yag shas
38b5(tsha): mchum zang //khal rag sha ru nu ma ma ti ri dzang //'di yis las rnams kyang ni 'grub//dbang gi rgyal pos dka' stsal pa//sring ba'i rgyal mo khyod kyis//'di'i las rnams grub par bgyi ces bsgo'o//rta mchog rol por tanA las/rnal po

39a1(tsha): @#dang blagam gru gsum brgyad rnams la//khyi brgyad rim par dgod par bya//gru gsum re la thun phor//dug dang khrag dang rdzas rnams dang //nas mthu can gyis 'bru yis dgang //de la snyen cing bsgrub par bya//a~M ku ku rag/

39b1(tsha): 'dus pa'i gnas chen du/de ru bsgrub pa chen po ni/long ba'i tshogs rnams bsgrub par bya//gru gsuM zla gam dbu su ste//'tha' ma zlum po thod pas bskor//de'i tham pad+ma zla gas bzhi//de'i mtha' ma pad+mak+Sha ma sum cu gnyis//dbu su gtso dang bla gam /

39b4(tsha): 'gri rba dang paM laM rbad/mu la she rbad/ke to lang rbad/mu sa ling /ba ma thum/ku laM ma bu she rbad/drung tod yam/sa ma traM rbad/ra sho ka/mu pu li sang /kar she ram/ru tra raM sha/mu lo rbad/khA ru traM/sa sho pa li rbad//pra du ni rbad/kuM kar nang /ro sad
39b5(tsha): mu li rbad/pra dre kha laM/kha la me tri rbad/pa r-i tri gum/g+ha ro ba tri ling /pra tod kha la/de ltar gsang sngags sgo su phule//drag sngags de yis thad 'grub//gsad dang bso la the tshom med//rta mchog rol pa'i tan+na las/long ba sum cu rtsa gnyis

39b7(tsha): de'i phyi rim pad+ma zla gi bzhi ru bya//khyams dang sgo dang sgo khyud bcas/dbus su dbang gi rgyal po ste//zla gam bzhi la yu bzhi dgod//bon da mtshan can bzhi la bsgrub/sgo bzhi khro bo mgo rtsigs bsrung //de ru gsang thun thong par bsag/sha khrag bcud
40a1(tsha): @#dang rtsi bcud dang /mthun pa'i rdzas la mchod cing bsgrub/gsang ba'i yum bzhi thod par 'bang /oM ma mA du shan shan ma zad/ma ma rag+ho ro shan rbang /ra go pa la tshal du yam/sa ma ka ra ma ma ti rbad/de ltar mchod bskal lnga/

40a5(tsha): g.yab mo dang /rgyu ma'i 'phreng dang lha gdugs dang /ha ta dang ni cha lang dang /ban da rdzas rnams tshogs par bsgrub ces gsung so//rta mchog rol pa'i tan+t+ra las/go la dang khram brgya mo bsgrub pa'i le'u ste don drug pa'o////de nas yang dbang gi rgyal po la/

40b4(tsha): ni ma chus rlung na mkha' lnga//'byung ba lnga'i yum du byin brlabs rtsi yino//bdud ni nyon mongs lnga yin te//yis lnga ru brlabs pa rtsi yino//a ti ma tri 'chi ba med pa'i don//bdud rtsi ar+m+mi ta zhes bya//de la blangs na sku gsung thugs dang yon tan 'phrin

48b3(tsha): su 'dus pa'o//rnar mi rtog ye nas skye med cing //gzung 'dzin skyon bral mtha' gnyis ma chags dbyings//ye nas mnyam nyid rang bzhin ji bzhin dang //rnam rtog skyon bral smra bsam yul las 'das pa ste//de ni byang chub sems
48b4(tsha): chen po/yod pa dang ni med pa dang /rtag pa dang ni chad pa dang //dngos po dang ni mtshan ma dang //gzung ba dang ni 'dzin pa brgyad//spros pa'i mtha' brgyad sems kyi sgo mo ste//de ltar gnas pa'ang ni sems kyi laso//sems kyi yul ni rnams
48b5(tsha): shes brgyad//sems kyi 'bras bu sangs rgyas//sems kyi btsal sbyong bsgrub pa'o//sems kyi gnya' gnon dam tshig/lta ba sdeng dang ldan par bal+la//bsgom pa sems kyi srog shing yin//bad skyed dang thad bsdud//thad cig pa
48b6(tsha): mas nyid do//de ltar byang chub sems chen po//yod par snang ba byang chub sems//chad par snang bar byang chub sems//gnyis ka yang ni sems nyidado//gnyis med dang ni sems nyid do//sems nyid la yang dngos po med//gang du
48b7(tsha): yang ma sbas pas mthong ba nyid do//brjod rdul tsam yang med pas kun tu khyab pa nyid do//ma shes 'phrul pas sen no//ci yang med pas shes rab bo//bgrod du med pas mthar thug go//byang chub sems ni mye long lha//ma nor ma btson
49a1(tsha): de bzhin te//rang byung rang bzhi de nyid do//skad cig ting 'dzin byang chub sems kyis don//rig pas mkha' la rlung gis sprin chags ltar//sngon nas bsags pa'i bag chags sel//dper na mun khud
49a2(tsha): smon bteg ltar//mun nyid snang bar gyur pa bzhin//byang chub sems kyi don rig pas//'dzin rtog kun kyang byang chub sem su gsal//yod mapa' med mtha' di ru rdzogs//rdzogs pa chen po zhes kyang bya//de ltar gnas de lta ba che//skyeng
49a3(tsha): pa ma btsal yangs pa che//byang chub sems chen kun tu bzang //kun bzangs yang ba chos kyi dbyings//thad ma lus rdzogs pa che//de ltar gnas pa phun sum pa de//sems kyi sangs rgyas bde ba la//sprul pa phyogs bcu
49a4(tsha): sad+yas nyan/ces gsung so//rta mchog rol pa'i tan+t+ra las//chos tha~Md dbang gi zhing du yongasu dag pa dang semasu bstan pa'i le'u ste go gnyis pa'o///de nas yang ni bshad bya ba//las kyi seng 'chen po nyin//thad dpa
49a5(tsha): du bsdu ba na//zla gam gdengs pa'i dkyil 'khor du//dbang gi lha rnams tshang bar dgod//dbang gi las kyis thad 'gugs shing sgral//gdug pa shing gi phur pa dgod//tho ba lcags rtsa dgra sta bsnams/gzhan yang dbang gi dam
49a6(tsha): dam dgram la//de'i las kyis thod 'gugs//spyan drangs bzhugs dang phyag 'tshal mchod//smon lam btab la 'dod don gsol//'gugs pa'i lha rnams gsal ba dang //bstod bcol las la bsgo ba'o//dbang
49a7(tsha): bdud las kyi ting 'dzin du bzhugs pa yis//srid pa'i dbang chen rnams kyang las la brtson//sum brgya drug bcu ka'i las la brtson//'brel pas gnyan pa'i 'khor du grags//zhes gsung so//rta mchog rol pa'i tan+ta wa/
49b1(tsha): 'khor mkha 'gro dbang du bsdu ba'i le'u ste go gsum pa'o///de nas bcom ldan 'das yang gi rgyal po badz+ra he ru ka la//bad+ga las kyi sems dpas 'di skad ces gsol to//bcom ldan dbang mchog dpa
49b2(tsha): gi rgyal nyam 'arungs gdugs pa klu gnyan 'di//gdug pa che zhing ma rungs pa//'byung po phal cher kun gyis rten//'byung po kun gyi brten yin pas//'di la gdul thabs ji ltar lags//dbang chen 'dus pas bka' stsol cig/
49b3(tsha): ces smras//de nas dbang gi rgyal po de//gsol ba'i don thugasu chung nas//ting 'dzin 'bum phrag gI nang nas//dbang bsdus kyi ting 'dzin dang //'joms byed 'bar bu'i tI 'dzin dang //mi g.yo ba'i ting dzinA dang //gzhan gyis rdzu 'phrul

49b6(tsha): kun tu snang ba'i ting 'dzin dang //ma nor rgyu yi ting 'dzin dang //bdal pa chen po'i ting 'dzin dang /thu gas rjes mi gtong ba'i ti 'dzin dang //sprul pa gsal ba'i ting 'dzin dang //snying rje chen po'i ting 'dzin dang /rang nga 'dren pa'i ting
49b7(tsha): 'dzin tete/de'i nang nas klu rgyal rdzu 'phrul dbrog pa//gdug pa can tshar gcad pa zhes bya ba'i ti nge 'dzin la snyoms par zhug so//zhugs ma thag tu gru de dag zad mdangangs nyams//rdzu 'phrul klu'i gdugs pa thad chad de bya rgyu med/rdzu
50a1(tsha): 'phrul bad 'phrogs te nyams par gyur to//de nas yang slar dran pa rnyed nas//bcom ldan 'das la 'di skad ces gsol to//gdug pa klu'i bu 'di la//'di lta bu'i las 'di byung ba ci//zer nas yang lus
50a2(tsha): 'di zad gdug pa'i rdzu 'phrul gyi mgo stong lnga brgya rang gis bsgreng ba'o//de nas dbang gi rgyal po des//ting 'dzin de les bzhings nas//drag po'i sgrol sngags 'di brjod//oM nA ga dung phyung nA ga dung phyur/a lo ka ta ya pra be sha phaT/
50a3(tsha): ba ga ban/ba su ban da pra pe sha phaT/ba ga ban/g+hi ba ro sha pra be sha phaT/oM ru ru sU ru ta sha ma dur ka ti bi tsa b+haM shi laM ru ru dra e ha hi sa ma ya ma tang bra be sha s+tha tang dza le yaM ha ha he he hUM phaT/oM ba hU bin danA ga bin du na ga hI ya/
50a4(tsha): nA ga du du pra be sha phaT/ha he hUM pra be sha phaT ba gaM ban/sti sti pra be sha phaT ba ga ban/rag+ma pra be sha phaT ba ga ban/n+da ga le nA ga le/dum ba le dum ba le/a ka le a ka le/spr-i ta le s+pho ta laM//a ger ba in tra pra be sha/
50a5(tsha): phaT/du sum du sram pram pram phaT/nA ga kar sha tig sna hU ya ba me ru ni kar sha ye/nA ga pram pram shag/nA ga ge raM sad bra ge ma ta nA ga hu ra pra be sha ya dza/zhes brjod pas/klu de dag thaMd bsgral ste/phung po zhabs dmar

50a7(tsha): rgyan du bor ro//de nas klu de rnams slar sos pa'i gzugs chen klu rnams//phyag bcu drug gi g.yas pa brgyad kyi dang por dga' bo gzung ngo //gnyis par nye dga' bo bzung ngo //gsum par klu'i rgyal po stobs ldan bcu
50b1(tsha): bzhi par legs ldan bzung ngo //snga bar sogs ma med bzung ngo //drug par mtha' ma so//bdun par drang shing ngo //brgyad par bad+ma'o//g.yon pa brgyad du//dang por mgo dgu pa'o//gnyis par mgo lnga pa'o//gsum par chu b+hA'o//
50b2(tsha): bzhi par kar ko na'o//lnga par ba ru na'o//drug par klu gdol pa'o//bdun par klu mgo gsum pa'o//brgyad par gdol pa nag po'o//de rnams phyag mtshan du bsnams pa'o//bar bar na yang klu bran 'jus pa yin//de nas yang klu
50b3(tsha): rnams thag bcad cing dbang du bsdus ste//bshog pa g.yas g.yon gnyisu gdengs//bar bar zla ba dang nyi ma stong stong gis brgyan//gshog pa'i bar re re nas//gnam ljags kyi mchu dang //lcags kyi sder mor byung //gshog
50b4(tsha): pa g.yas g.yon gyi rtse mo nas//sprul pa'i bya khyud re re tib pa yod pa/byu ru'i rkang la lcags kyi sder mor yod pa'o//dmig me dang chur yod pa//de ltar sprul nas/gdug pa'i klu tha~Md gzung zhing zoso//pags pas rgyal mtshan byas//
50b5(tsha): sbrul mgo thod bregs nas gshog pa la brgyan pa/gti mug dag par byas pa'o//de nas yang klu genyan tha~Md sdigs pa'i sngags ni dbang gi rgyal po'i sku dang gsung las mtho na//oM nA gi pa sha he ra te shag tshal bu ram sad/zhes
50b6(tsha): gsung so//de nas yang klu gnyan tha~Md bsgral ba'i sngags 'di mthon te/oM nA ga he sa pa ro dum pa le sad/pram mi sham sad/nA ga he raM du na mu kha lo rbad//ma rA ling sad na rbad ya//de ni gsang pa'i sngagasu gsungs/de nas yang klu
50b7(tsha): gnyen de rnams gso bo'i sngags 'di mngon to//nA ga pa sha yu ti pa la a/klu rnams gso ba'o//de nas yang klu dang 'byung po tshar bcad pa'i sngags 'di gsungaso//oM pa sha nA ga dril ma ta pa sha ga ge na sa ma ya swA hA/oM sa te/
51a1(tsha): @#par ti her sa sh+ma/tra h+ya ya/thaMd tshar gcad bsrung ba'o//de ltar dbang gi rgyal po pad+ma dbang chen des/gdugs pa'i tshogs tshar gcad de bsgral nas//la la gdan du bor/la ya phyag mtshan du
51a2(tsha): bsnams pa'o//la ya rgyal mtshan la btsugs pa'o//la la las la btsud pa'o//de nas gsos nas slar bkod pa'o//de ltar bsrung ba dang tshar bcad pa dang /sprul pa yan chad dbang du bsdus te//klu rgyal 'bum gyis dbang
51a3(tsha): gi sgo gsungs pa'o//klu dang gnyen gyi sde rnams kyang /shin tu ston pas gsungs pa la rjes yi rang nas/mgu ba dang rdzas par gyur nas khyab pa nas kun tu ston pa la bsten te gnas pa'o//rta mchog rol pa'i tan+t+ra las/klu
51a4(tsha): gnyen dbang du sdus shing bsgral nas les la sgo ba de tshar bcing bsrung ba'i sngags gsungs pa'i le'u ste go bzhi pa'o///de nas dbong gi rgyal po pad+ma he ru ka la/las kyi rgyal po dang /pad+ma sems
51a5(tsha): dpa' dang /phyag na rje dang gsum gyis nus shing don bstan la dbab pa ste//de nas phyag na rjes 'di skad ces gsol to//kye ma sang+yas 'dus pa'i yab//sang+yas kun gyi dgongs pa'i mchog/bcom ldan yab la bdag nyan 'tshal//'dus
51a6(tsha): bas chos kyi rnams grangs gsungs chos gsol to//de nas bcom ldan dbang mchog des//skye mched chos kyi dbyings chen nas//'gag pa med pa'i skur mtshon nas//sbrul skus 'gro don mjed pa'o//de nas 'di skad ces bka'
51a7(tsha): swa kho/phyag na rdo la sogs nyon//chos kyi rnam grangs dbye bar bya//chos kyi rnam grangs dbye ba la//de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyis/thugs kyi dgongs pa bsam mi khyab//thugs kyi namakha'i dbyings dang 'dra//na mkha'i dbyings
51b1(tsha): dpag tu med//dpag med bsam las 'das pa'o//thugs ni dpag par dka' ba yin//sku dang gsung yang de bzhin//de bzhin gshegs pa'i dgongs pa yang //dpag par rga bar gsungs pa te/de bzhin gsung gi chos 'di la/sku dang
51b2(tsha): gsung dang thugs 'di la/de'i rnam pa bshad pa ste//dbye ba'i rnam grangs dpag dka' yang //bsdun cig las med par gsungs//bsam du med pa'i chos nyid la//ma nor rtogs na bsgom pa yin//ma nor ba dang ma bcos pa//ngang la gnas
51b3(tsha): ni sgom par bstan//lta ba mnor rjeng du bzhag/bsgom pa ma bcos ma yongs pa//phrin las rol pa ma 'gag pa'o//yon tan zad med gter dang ldan//gsang sngags bsgrub par bstan pa 'di//lta ba dang ni spyod pa dang /dbang dang dam tshig/
51b4(tsha): shes par gces//sangs rgyas 'dod pas stong par sgom//stong pa'i ngang la ye shes skye//skye ba med pa'i chos nyid la//'gag pa med pa'i ye shes shar//skye 'gag med par chos kyi sku//chos kyi sku yang sems
51b5(tsha): yin te/sems nyid ye shes shar ba'o//na mikha' gar khyab sems nyid de//sems kyis gar khyab na mkha' yin/cir snang cir srid cir bsgoms kyang //sems los ma rdzogs ye shes med//thaMd sems nyid ngang du shar//

51b7(tsha): gsang ba yang ni brjod pa yin//byang chub sems ni smad byung //rmang byung byang chub sems chen la//rig pa dbye brdal chen po yin//sems nyid rang 'byung les yin//rig pa'i ye shes klong brdal bas//brdal ba chen po chos
52a1(tsha): gang /dang la mi g.yo bsgom pa yin//de las bsgom du med pa ste/nga las ma yengs gnas par bya//kun bzang bde chen lhun grub pa//rig pa'i yis sems nyid de//ma bsgoms ba nag na bsgos pa yin//ma tshal ba nyid rtsal
52a2(tsha): nyid yin ltar gnas ma yis/ces gan so//mchog ram pa'i rgyud chen polas// ba btsun pa'i len steg de rnaMs cang ni gsal ba'i nga//gang gis rgyal pos bka' stsal pa//phyag na rdo rje las kyi kyil/'du ma/
52a3(tsha): la sogs nyon/chos kyi rnam grangs bstan pa ni//byang chub kyi yan lag bdun la gom par bya'o//lam gyi 'bras bu thob pa'o//rdzu 'phrul gyis rkang pa bzhi bzung cig//skyon gyis ma gos pa thob pa'o//mngon par shes pa bsgoms pa bsnams cig//das
52a4(tsha): grub thob ciha nyes pa med pa'o//'phags pa'i lam yan lag brgyad pa bzung zhing rig par gyis shig/gol sa med pa thob pa'o//gsung rabs yan lag bcus rtogs shing spyod la rig par byos//'bras bu re dog med par thob pa'o//byang chub
52a5(tsha): phyogs kyi chos sum cu rtsa bdun nye bar spyod cig/'gag pa med pa'i 'bras bu thob pa'o//pha rol phyin pa bcu la spyod cig/thabs dang shes rab la sogs pa'i sgom pas 'bras bu ma spangs gnyis med pha rol phyin pa'o//pha rol phyin bcu ni/sbyin
52a6(tsha): pha rol tu phyin pa yis//chags med yis chen po thob//tshul khrims kyi pha rol tu phyin pa yis//bde ba'i nga gi yis thob//bzod pa'i pha rol phyin pa yis//mi g.yo ba'i les zo'o//brtson 'grus kyi pha rol tu phyin pa yis//bya ba nan tan yis thob//pas
52a7(tsha): gtan gyi pha rol tu phyin pa yis//gsal la rtog med yis thob//shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyin pa yis//me long lta bu'i yis thob//stobs kyi pha rol tu phyin pa yis//mnyam pa nyid kyi yis thob//smon laM gyi pha rol tu phyin pa yis//gsal la rtogs med

52b4(tsha): tota/oM ya ma he kra ma he ru na 'dun sa da raM raM raM/ba sha ru tri sa ma pra be sha phaT nan ya/ke la e ne seng ro/kha la dur sad ru tri pra ma ya//sa ma ya ba shaM ka ra phaT/ya ma staM pa pra be sha phaT//ka laM dun dun pra ma ya//mu du gla ling sad ha d+hi ga rbang /bam

52b6(tsha): ba ro sha tsa ling //tis sta ban da ban khang raM sang /nA ga ru ya pra sha sa~M/keng du wam na sham khyer ya/pra sha khaM sad lo bai ro ling ya/dang med kra mag se se sa na yam/dus pa ling du ba ling du ba long /pra dang cha la khal te sang laM/nA ga shag tshal/

53a1(tsha): @#mur khA ling nan/dun ni dun nA shag nA ga pe sa hu ra sa/de yis gdugs pa'i ku gnyan bsgral//de ma thag nas sngags 'di 'jod//as pa tsa la sham sa ling //lag shwA kha tha kha bram yang ya zang na ling /sa ma ya du krI he
53a2(tsha): sa prad ling la/mur tsa la yag sha rag sha staM ba ni/chal pa tshal pa/zad pu rngad pu/kha 'ang kha 'dar//mur grwa ying ya/de yis la/prA ka sha la sadad ni sa rbad ni ling /de yis gnod sbyin ma rungs sprul//de ma zag nas sngags 'di 'bon//oM sa
53a3(tsha): dupra ma ya ba//sha na na pra saM ya ko la he ra ya/tsiti ka la~M su ma kI ka ma rbad ya/kha la ru tri mA tsa kha la dun/shA lo bra ga la yang /daMr di ri/ratsu pra ya thums ya/praka sha la sad da ne sa rbad na ying /de yis gza' dang sker ma 'asgral ba yin/

53a6(tsha): ka du la ma la du ma yi/zhes rgyal po'i rigs mang bsgra 'o//de nas me nyid kyi sku dang gsung dang thugs da krugs pa de rnams mngon//oM tsa tsa da li sil laM/cag sha gur ba sha sa/mar bshan sa pa ya/se twe ba sha ma tsar yid la/k+Sha ta ka

53b1(tsha): tsa la ya/tsi ta pra sha gugs pa ling /sa to ru bi ti ling rbad ya/tsati mur tsa kha la rbang ya/de ni btsan rigs zad skal ba'o//sgrol sngags de rnams kyis/reg pa de dag thaMng bsgral nas/mu gya med par byaso//de byis kyang de bzhin du bsgral ba'o//
53b2(tsha): /sgrol ba'i sngags de zin pa yis//ma rus gdugs tshogs zad sbal zhes gsung so//rta mchog rol pa'i tAn+t+ra las/drag po bsgrol ba'i sngags thon pa'i le'u skye go bdun pa'o////de nas yang dbang gis rgyal po'i sku gsungs las/babyung po tri
53b3(tsha): dza ma ra ya dza/gam pa ri dza/pad+ma da ra hri ba mo ka hr-i/ha ya g+hi ba hri/sum ban ne hri/a mo ka hri/ha ya g+hi ba hr-i/gam da ni la rbad/b+han da 'gre na ro sha pa pa sho pa ri dza/sa ma ya khug/dam pa dum pa/ha ra re ho ra re/tsi le tsa le/gur ba gur ba/shaM ba ri
53b4(tsha): bag ka mang la hu ra ya/ta le tu le/ka sa ru bi 'gri ba ha be/de ni don gyis bon pa'o//'di ni brgyad kyi dreg sngags thon pa'o//ku ku ra ka sha kha la tshal tshal chem rbad/ku ku rag sha ya kha ya dum ling rbad//ku ku rag sha me ta me ta baM ril rbad/
53b5(tsha): ku ku rag sha hri dung dung ril rbad/ku ku rag sha hri kha long ka paM ril thad//ku ku rAg+sa ga shaM ki ra bo ril rbad//ku ku ruH hUM b+h+yo dum pa le ril rbad/ku ku rag kha long ka ka ma la ril rbad//de nas spyang brgyad kyi sngags mthon to/oM dza ya du khug
53b6(tsha): ling cad/sho na ka ka ling ling du khug ling rbad/tsag shag ku ku sho na rbad/mya ti kaM ling hUr zad/se ta se ta breng zad/kur shaM bam ling rbad//khu ye sha la marbad/mu ru kha sa tsaM ling zad/de nas yang long ba brgyad sbrel su nu rtsa gnyis kyi dra/

70b2(tsha): hUMtad ni gsung dag pa zhing lpags kyi dbang bsgyur ste//gsung rab mchog thabs dang ldan pas rtags mtshon pa//gsung rabs yan lag don las ma nyams bdas mtshon nas/brjid pas zhing lpags dbang bskur bas//thabs kyi pha rol byin pa
70b3(tsha): dbang thob shog/bam tig sna ma 'da' la a//da ni glang chen lpags pas dbang bskur te//bde ba chen po sku mtshon phyir//brjid chen dbang bsdud stobs dang ldan//sku'i phrin las rdzogs pa'i phyir//glang gi lpags pas dbang bskur bas//

70b5(tsha): gis lpags pas dbang bskur bas//smon lam pha rol pa'i dbang thob shog/baM tI ba kri kA bi tAts+tsha//da ni bas khrab srug pos dbang bsgyur bya ste//sku 'khregs rol pa'i rdzu 'phrul mnga' snyems pas//nA bza' brjid 'bar rol pa sku'i brgyan
70b6(tsha): du rme bsre khrab smug po dbang gis bskur byas pas//tshul khrims pha rol byin pa'i dbang thob shog//da te pa shad+d+ho/ni gdug bzlog khyod ljags kyi dbang bskur bar bya s+ho//'dzum bye sngon mo lcags kyi khrub//ye shes mtshan gyis de mo
70b7(tsha): min 'bum ze 'di yos dbang bskur bas//bsam gtan pha rol byin pa'i dbang thob shog/had ta ba sha hU~M/rna gdug bzlog byed lcags kyi dbang bskur bar bya s+ho//'bum bya sngan ma lcags kyi khra ba//ye shes mtshan gyis dal man/
71a1(tsha): @rang nA pra ne hUM/de nas gzigs gis dbang bsgyur bar bya ste//den phyir chags dang rtags chen 'joms//gdugs m+b+ha lus tshur bcang byer//gdun zan rtul po gtum pa gzigs gos rgyul//rtags kyis thig le 'di
71a2(tsha): yis dbang bskur bas//sbyin pa'i pha rol phyin pa'i dbang thob shog//bar nor pa ya/de nas dar gyis brgyan gyis dbang bsgyur bar bya ste/hUM'i bri nag lab rgan chu rgan dar gyis brgyan//chos spyi 'gyur med shes bya thogs pa med pa'i//mthing ka
71a3(tsha): lebargan dar gyis dbang bskur bas//shes rab pha rol phyin pa'i dbang thob shog/prad nya d+ha ra ma d+har du I/zhes gsung so//rta mtsho ga rol pa'i tan+t+ra las/brgyan dang na bzas dbang bskur ba'o le'u ste/brgya bcu gnyis pa'o//
71a4(tsha): de nas yang ston pas dbang bskur ba'i las 'di bka' stsal to//byang chub sems kyi pha rol tu phyin pa bcu'i dbang bskur ba ni//mthar thug gsang ba byang chub sems kyi dbang bskur ba//sems bskyed sngon du 'gro bas pha rol byin bcu'i
71a5(tsha): dbang bskur ba dang gsum mo//mthar thugs pa byang chub sems dpa' pha rol tu phyin pa bcu'i dbang bskur ba ni//byang chub sems yab yum brdas bskul ba bde ba bkye de'i bar ni ma chad pa//'dren lkugs dang dga'i nyi shu gcig/sa'i don
71a6(tsha): dang ldan pa'i dus//'phrul gyis phyis ma blangs pa tshe//de la sbyin dang bskyed byas la//sbyin dkar ting 'dzin gnyis med 'grod med don mtshon dang //de pa ldan pas gsal byas la//gsal ba'i bskul ba 'di rnams brjod pa'i dbang //nges

71b1(tsha): sbyin pa'i pha rol phyin pa'i dbang thob shog/byang chub sems ni tshul khrims ldan//tshul dang khrims ni 'bras bu gnas//tshul khrims pha rol phyin pa'i dbang thob shog/byang chub sems ni bzod pa'i mchog/sems kyi ngo bo kun kyang bzod //bzod //
71b2(tsha): pa sems nyid dang la gnas//bzod pa'i pha rol phyin pa'i dbang thob shog/byang chub sems ni brtson 'grus che//brtson la 'grus pa ye shes che//brtson 'grus pha rol phyin pa'i dbang thob shog/byang chub sems ni bsam pa brtan//bsam med
71b3(tsha): ngal gnas te pha rol phyin//bsam gtan pha rol phyin pa'i dbang thob shog/byang chub sems ni shes rab che//shes rab yis ting dpag don//gnyis ming rol po bde ba'i mchog/shes rab pha rol phyin pa'i dbang thob shog/byang chub
71b4(tsha): sems ni stobs po che///mi 'gyur chos nyid dag pa la//rtogs pas de nyid yin te de'i don dang ldan//stobs kyi pha rol phyin pa'i dbang thob shog/thabs dang shes rab 'brel pas na//shes rab mngon du thabs song bas//byang chub
71b5(tsha): sems ni thabs kyi don mthong ba//thabs kyi pha rol phyin pa'i dbang thob shog/byang chub sems ni smon pa'i lam/smon lam dag pa chos nyid don//smon lam pha rol phyin pa'i dbang thob shog/byang chub sems ni snying rje che/snying rje'i lam mchog
71b6(tsha): /chos nyid don//snying rje pha rol phyin pa'i dbang thob shog/ma bgrod don la pha rol phyin//ci la ma g.yos thams cad snang //pha rol phyin bcu ye shes dbang thob shog//be de tsi ti ma hA su gA A A oM hU~M/rta mchog ril po'i tan tra las//gsang ba sh+ka
71b7(tsha): rab ye shes kyi byang chub sems la rol tu phyin pa bcu'i dbang gis le'u ste brgya bcu gsum pa'o///de nas yang phyag na rdo rjes/bcom ldan 'das dbang gi rgyal po la 'di skad ces gsol to//kye ma dbang chen kun
73a1(tsha): @ldan dbang chen rgyal ba'i mchog/rigs chen gsang ba'i bdag po yin//rigs bsdus gang sang chen 'dus pa yis//sku gsung thugs kyi dngos grub gsol//chos sku gsal la rtogs med dngos grub gsol/hUM bde/
73a2(tsha): gshegs rgyal ba ma lus ste//chos sku zhi ba'i ngang las skye ba med pa las//skye ba gzugs sku phyag rgya chen por bzhugs pa'o//rdo rje dbyings kyi dkyil 'khor yang /tshe dang ye shes dngos grub stsol//ye shes dbyings las 'bar ba'i skur bstan
73a3(tsha): pa tarta mgrin rgyal po 'khor bcas kyang //tshe dbang ye shes tshogs pa'i dngos grub stsol//rig pa thugs kyi gzhol yas su//nyi zla 'od kyi bdan stengs nas//ye shes sems dpa' bka'i gnas//'phro 'du dgongs pa tshang lags kyi///
73a4(tsha): 'bum phrag ting 'dzin spyod pa'i dngos grub stsol//thugs mchog zhi ba'i ngang las ma g.yos kyang //'bar ba'i cha lugs bzhugs pa ste//khrag mthung rol pa phyag brgya 'bar ba'i tshogs//'gro ba 'dul ba'i lha dang lha mo'i tshogs//khams gsuM
73a5(tsha): dbang du bsdus nas zhi ba'i ngos grub gsol//tshe dang ye shes byang chub mchog gi dngos grub gsol//gdugs pa'i tshigs rnams ma lus kun gul nas//bdud las rgyal ba'i rgyal thabs bdud rtsi bsnams//mgu sbyin longs spyod ngas/
73a6(tsha): grub stsal du gsol//ngan song gnas rnams ma lus but+tAl nas kyang //byang chub bde ba'i gnas la 'god pa//gnyis med bde ba'i dngos grub stsal du gsol//dur 'a khrod 'bar ba'i chas dang rab ldan pa//dur khrod chen po'i dkyil
73a7(tsha): 'khor gdug pa 'dul//rigs pa mthu'i dngos grub stsal du gsol//byang chub sems ni rigs pa'i gnas//hUM mdzad chas ldan dur khrod brgyad dang ldan//khrag 'thung rol pa'i dngos grub stsal du gsol//be ka'i pho nya'i thogs med gdug
73b1(tsha): /pa 'dub ba'i tshogs//myur mgyogs longs spyod dngos grub stsal du gsol//ye shes bzhi las gdug pa'i sku sprul nas//phrin las rgya mtsho 'gro don mthar ru phyin//rigs pa'i rtul shugs nyon mongs tha ba 'joms//rdzu 'phrul drag pos
73b2(tsha): gang la re'i phrin las ston//don las mi nyams byang chub snying por gnas//de ring brtul shugs mthu'i dngos grub gsal du gsol//dbyings las 'bar ba'i skur bstan nas//mkha' gro mi zad pa'i las kyi mtha' bsdud pa/ci 'dod phun sum
73b3(tsha): tshogs pa'i dngos grub stsol//ye shes dbyings las skyed pa'i ma ma'i chags//thugs kyi phrin las sna tshogs mi dmigs kyang //gdug pa 'dul bying ma mo'i sku sprul nas//phrin las myur mgyogs po nya mang po 'gums//mthu
73b4(tsha): sbal gyis dngos grub gsol//las las grub dang ma mo mkha' gro dang /'jig rten nor dang longs spyod dang //tshe ring nang med bde skyid phun sum tshogs//gang la des 'dul tshe'i dngos grub gsol//dag pa byang chub dngos grub stsal//dgas
73b5(tsha): pa gang la gang 'dod pa//nor bu rin chen dpe bzhin dngos grub gsol//ces gsung so//rta mchog rol pa'i tan+t+ra las//dbang gi lha rnams la dngos grub spang ba'i le'u ste brgya bco lnga pa'o///de nas yang dbang gi rgyal
73b6(tsha): bo kun 'dus kyis//dbang gi lha rnams la bskang ba'i las nyams chags bshags pa dang //bskang pa 'di gsung so//'dab kyi bya dmar sgro dang /shel sdug 'tshems khams dang //de yis thugs dam bskang ba ste//nyams pa chags pa
73b7(tsha): bskang ba na//dngos grub dpa bskongs par 'gyur//rtsa brgyad stong las sbyar ba'i bdud rtsi dang /rdo rje 'das ma phag pa'i mngal khrag dang /thig le zags pa'i khrag chen dang //sha khrag sbyar ba'i gtor ma dang //rgya mchog dang ni mkha' lding
74a1(tsha): dang ka 'di yis thugs dam bskang byas pa//pang ma rigs kyi thugs dam bskongs gyur cig/'di yis yid dang kun bzhi yis//dbang chen yab yum thugs dam bskang //'di'i gsang ba'i sbugs khrag gis//
74a2(tsha): mgrin rgyal po'i thugs dam bskang //'di la rtogs tshogs bzhi bcu gnyis//rdo dbyings kyi dkyil 'khor lha tshogs thugs dam bskang //'di'i gzugs kyi phung po yis//rnam par snang mdzad thugs dam bskang //'di'i tshor ba'i phung po
74a3(tsha): yis//rin chen 'byung ldan thugs dam bskang //'di'i 'du shes phung po yis//snang ba mtha' yas thugs dam bskang //'di'i 'du byed phung po yis//don yod grub pa'i thugs dam bskang //'di'i rnam shes phung po yis//rdo rje sems
74a4(tsha): dpa'i bugs dam bskang //'di'i 'byung ba lnga rnams kyis//sangs rgyas yum lnga'i thugs dam bskang //'di'i rnam par shes pa'i tshogs brgyad kyis//byang chub sems pa brgyad kyis thugs dam bskang //'di'i rnam par shes pa'i
74a5(tsha): yul brgyad kyis//byang chub sems ma brgyad kyis thugs dam bskangs//'di'i yang dang chos rnams kyis//kun tu bzang po yab yum thugs dam bskung //'di'i nyon mongs drug rnams kyis//bcom ldan thub pa drug gi thugs
74a6(tsha): dam bskang //'di'i rtag chad bzhi rnams kyis//sgo ba yab yum thugs dam bskang //'di'i 'od phung lnga po yis//ye shes sems dpa'i dam bskang //'di'i phung po lnga po gnas 'gyur bas/khrag mthung he ru ka'i thugs dam bskad/
74a7(tsha): 'di'i 'byung ba lnga po gnas 'gyur bas//khrag 'thung yum lnga'i thugs dam bskang //'dir rnam par shes pa'i tshigs brgyad gnas 'gyur bas//ki ri khri mo brgyad kyi thugs rim skang //'dri'i yul brgyad gnas 'gyur bas//
74b1(tsha): sing nga 'phra men brgyad kyis thugs dam skang //'di'i rtag chad bzhi po gnas 'gyur bas//sgro ma bzhi'i thugs dam bskang //'di'i gzhung bu nyi shu rtsa brgyad kyis//gser gyi seng ge'i nyi shu rtsa brgyad thugs
74b2(tsha): dam bskang dang 'gro ba 'dul b+haSh+Ti thugs dam bskang /'di'i rtsib ma g.yas g.yon dang /'di'i bshog pa g.yas g.yon gyis//khams sum rnar rgyal gyi thugs dam bskang /'di'i mdangs chen bzhi po yis/gsang ba'i
74b3(tsha): yum bzhi thugs dam bskang //'odi'i rtsa sgo rngubs chen gyis/dur khrod hUM mdzad chen po'i dkyil 'khor thugs dam bskang //'di'i lte ba sprul pa yis//ngan song sbyong ba'i dkyil 'khor thugs dam bskang //'di'i dur khrod sha khrag
74b4(tsha): gis//dur khrod chen po'i dkyil 'khor thugs dam bskang //'di'i bcud ni bzhi po yis//brtul shugs bzhi'i thugs dam skang //'di'i khrag bcud snying mchin gyis//rta'i brtul shugs thugs dam bskang //'di'i rus bcud
74b5(tsha): rlung tshil gyis//khyi'i brtul shugs thugs dam bskang //'di'i sems bcud klad ba yis//bya'i brtul shugs thugs dam bskang //'di'i sha bcud tshil bu yis//phag gi brtul shugs thugs dam bskang //'di'i dung
74b6(tsha): khrod smad lnga yis//spyang mo brgyad kyis thugs dam bskang //'di'i mkhar bu rtsigs pa yis//long pa brgyad 'brel sum cu rtsa gnyis thugs dam bskang //'di'i khrag mtsho dngas ma yis//mkha'agro ma tshogs thugs dam bskang //
74b7(tsha): 'di'i yan lag sde bzhi yis//mgo rtsegs khro bo'i thugs dam bskang //'di'i dngas mA chen dang //'di'i rtsad+ha srog sbubs kyis//si pad bcu gsum thugs dam bskang //ces gsung so//rta mchog rol pa'i tan tra las/dbang gi
75a1(tsha): @lha rnams thugs bskang ba'i le'u ste brgya bcu drug pa'o///de nas yang dbang gi rgyal pos bka' stsal pa//de nas dbang gi phur pa bshad par bya//yang dag sems kyi phur pa de/gzhan

75a6(tsha): thugs kyi sprul pa he ru bzhi//gsal bar bskyed cing gdab pa'o//dug lnga rtsad nas bcad pa'i phyir//sprul pa he ru bzhis kyang gdab//bsgral nas gdab dang slar gsol ba'o//oM pad+ma ki la ya/A mo kI la ya/rtsa rtsa ma ma ki li Sha/

76a7(tsha): mthum sau ra~M g+ni pu ma khe he re/hUMg yis g.yon zangs de shes rab 'brel pa'i klong dkil du/khar su rgyal ba'i dkyil 'khor ma lus la/dam rdzas sbyin sreg 'bar ba'i mchong ba 'phrul//dmigs pa brlag cing 'khor ba dang/
76b1(tsha): /song stongs par mdzod//snum pa gnan kro mar ya kha ra kha li/rig btsu n+ya rtsol 'khor du//hUM mdzad chen po'i dkyil 'khor ma lus la//drag po sbyin sreg 'bar ba'i mchod pa 'bul//dmigs pa brtan nas 'pho ba'i las mdzod cig/ ling /

76b3(tsha): par mdzod//oM rag sha kro d+ha ma ra ya kha hi raM/6/de ltar mchod pa phul ba dang /dmigs pa ga go bsreg pa dang //drag po smon lam gdab pa dang //'phrin las rnam bzhi rdzogs pa dang /thabs la dbang ba'i bro yang rdung //de yis las

76b5(tsha): ru dra sgral ba'i sha khrag de//btab dang sbyin sreg bya ba'o//btab pa 'di ltar dbang gi lha rnams bstab so//ma dang chen po sprul pa 'dis//thugs rnam gnyen po 'dis bskong so//'di'i kun gzhi gnas chen 'di//dbang gi rgyal po nyid
76b6(tsha): btab te 'di'i gtsug gi phung ka 'dis//rta mgrin rgyal po nyid la btab//'di'i bcud dang 'od tshogs kyis//rdo dbyings kyi zhal du bsgrub/'di'i 'od chen mdangs ma 'di//ye shes sems dpa' nyid la btab//'di'i
76b7(tsha): sha khrag phung po 'di/'gro ba 'dul ba'i zhal du btab//'di'i sbrul chen gzhung bu 'di//khams sum rgyal po nyid la btab//'di'i zhabs ni g.yas g.yon 'di//hUM mdzad chen po'i 'khor la btag Ta 'di'i yid ni nag po 'dis/
76b8(tsha): a ba chen rigs bsdus rgyabor du//dang gi lha tshogs bcas pa la/dam rdzas sbyin srog 'bar ba'i mchod pa 'di'i bul bas//lce rnams mnyes shing gdugs pa 'joms par shig/shri rdzi dbang po mo ta tsu sA
77a1(tsha): ngan song skyong ba'i lha la btab//'di'i dur khrod nang grol gyis//dur khrod chen po'i lha la btab//gsang ba'i rigs can 'di dag gis//lhan gyis grub pa'i lha la btab//'dar yan lag dpung bzhi yang/
77a2(tsha): rtsub zhugs chen po bzhi la bstag//'di'i nang grol smad lda ni//mkha' 'gro po nya'i tshogs la btab/sha dang khrag dang rus pa 'di//drug gsum bsgral ba'i mchod pa 'di//ma mo mkha' gro tshogs la btag/ces gsung so//
77a3(tsha): rje mchog rol pa'i tan+t+ra yas/btab pa'i las kyi rim pa'i le'u ste/brgya bcu dgu pa'o///denas lhag ma ga go la/bro brdungs pa 'di gsung so//sangs rgyas rgyal ba rnams kyi bka'/
77a4(tsha): gar gyi dbang phyug chen po des//rAg ma lus bsgral ba las//phung po lhag ma ga gau la//'dul bya'i blo cig brdung ba ni//bro ni brtul shugs chen po bzhi//rnam bzhi rdzogs pa'i brdungs pas//las rnams thaMd grub pa'i
77a5(tsha): khro/rigs bzhi khrag 'thung 'khrigs pa'i blo//bde gshegs rgyal ba dgongs pa'i bro//ma sringad rgya mtsho gdungs pa'i bro//ngan song 'khor ba 'joms pa'i bro//dug lnga gnasu dag pa'i bro//dug gsum dbyingasu bsdud pa'i
77a6(tsha): bro/sangs rgyas dgongs pa grub pa'i bro//nyon mongs gdung ba sel ba'i bro//rta'i bro ni nub nas brdung //khyi'i bro ni lho nas brdung //phrag gi blo ni shar nas brdung //bya'i bro ni byang nas brdung //cho ga chen po bzhi bcas nas/
77a7(tsha): dang po bro'i gdangs bton pas//dus gsum rgyal ba'i thugs dam bskul//bar du blo'i gdengs ston pas//bsgrub pa dag la dngos grub 'debs//tha mar bro'i gdangs sgra yin//'khor ba ngan song gtan nas sgrol//
77b1(tsha): brdungs pas byang sems 'phro 'dur 'khrigs/skye 'gag med pa'i dbyings rum du//blangs pa rgyal ba'i thugs dam bskul//'khor ba'i skam gling chen po la//phyogs bcu'i sang+yas ram zlar gnyer//ye shes phag gi pan ka
77b2(tsha): cas//ka ru dpon ye shes lnga'i mdzod//kun tu bzang po'i mkha' dbyings la//khamasu yongs sgrol chen po mdzod//lhag ma rjen shul dag la ni//brtul shugs chen po bro brdungs pas//dug gsum brtsad nas
77b3(tsha): bcod pa'i bro//rta'i brtul shugs bro brdungs pas//srid gsum ma lus dbang du bsdung //khro bi rdo rje ga gon pas//rta skad rngam pa'i ngang las rol//shangs kyi rlung bsgom chen po nas//rdo rje mtshon lnga char phab pas//dbang gi
77b4(tsha): rta bo brdungs pa yis//dang po ga go zhom par byas//bar me lhag ma rol bar byas//tha mas gdug rtsub byang bar byas//lhag ma med par rdul du rlags//dug gsum gnasu stongs par byas//rta'i dbu gtsug rum nas//
77b5(tsha): de'i pho nya skar tshogs spros//mtshon skor skar ltar 'khrugs pa'i bro//drag po gtum po lhan ner mdzod//thugs kha'i yi ge hri 'phros pas//sbrul sku dbang du bsdud pa'i bro//lhag ma med par gdar sha tshong //lho nas khyi'i
77b6(tsha): bo brdungs pas//brtul shugs chen po las kyi bro//drag po gtum po dbang du bsdus//phyogs bzhi'i 'tshams bzhi brgyad dag du//sprul pa khyi'i bro brdungs pas//thams cad tshar bcad bde la bkod //bde gshegs
77b7(tsha): rnams kyi thugs dam bskangs//'khor ba ngan song dbying su bcom//byang nas byi'i bri brdungs pas//srid gsum khams sum zil gyis mnan//klu la sogs pa'i gdug pa rnams//zas su za shing rngam stabs byed//
78a1(tsha): chu drug yongs su 'debs par byed//sprul pas yongs su lding bar byed//sprul pa mi zad ma rungs pa//la la lding dang la la 'phrag la la blan la rtog pa'i tshul//la la ldings shing la la 'jun
78a2(tsha): kha dog sna lnga 'bar ba la//mi 'thun gdug pa so sor 'dul//sprul pa bye ba las la 'byed//gdugs pa gtum po gtan nas 'joms//dbang gi bro ga yongasu gding //lhag ma rjes shul med pa'i bro//rgyal ba rnams
78a3(tsha): kyis thugs ngam skyongs//bro ga yongasu gding bar gyis//shar nas phag gi brtul shug byung //gti mug 'joms pa'i las la mkhas//byung dang ngor med drag po ste//sra sI med par 'joms pa'i bro//phag gi brtul
78a4(tsha): shugs chen po yin//zas ni gtsang sme med par za//bang ni ngal ngal dub med par rgyug/tshems ni sra snyi med par 'joms//'khron du ma gyur gang yang med//srid pa'i 'khron rko nga re mkhas//nyon mongs tha ba gtan nas
78a5(tsha): 'joms//'dod chags rgya mtsho gting nas skems//nga rgyal brag ri rtse nas snyil//'phra dog zug du gtan nas chom//gti mug mun pa gtan nas chom//zhe sdang rtsa me rum du song //zhes gsungs//rta mchog rol ba'i tan+t+ra
78a6(tsha): las/lhag ma'i rjes shul bro brdungs pa'i le'u ste/brgya nyi shu pa'o///de nas dbang gi rgyal po las 'di dag gsung so//gsang ba'i dngos grub bsgrub 'dod na//rta mgrin rta'i rgyal po la/
78a7(tsha): sprul pa hi ru bzhi bskur ba//de ni yang dag bslab par bya//de'i yon tan rdzogs pas ni//'di'i mthu dang rdzu 'phrul dngos grub thob//byang chub snying po 'grub par the tsom med //rigs dang mthun pa'i gnas dag tu/
78b1(tsha): phun sum tshogs pa'i bsgrub pa des//las kyi rim pa gzhung bzhin bya//dkyil 'khor zla gam sgo gcig pa//ba sa zla gam bzhis bskor ba//sgo dang chas par bya ba'o//chag chag thig gang ba bri ba dang //rdzas dang ldan par bsham/
78b2(tsha): pa'o//gdug shing bur pa lnga bsog te//byin gyis brlabs pa byas nas ni//zla gam lta dgod par bya//mthun pa'i phyi nang gsang ba'i rdzas//bdud rtsi gtor ma bzhin du te//ma dad pa de bsgral ba las//brdul la sogs pa'i
78b3(tsha): chu gsum gyis//lha rnams sku'i khrus su gsol//byang chub sems dang dri chen dang /maM sa dang ni rak+ta dang //dri chu la sogs ra sa ya na la sogs pas//bdud rtsi dam pa sman sbyar nas//lha rnams kun la dbul bar bya/
78b4(tsha): ma dad pa de bsgral ba'i//dbus ni snying la mtha' ma rtsi be ma ste//sha dang khrag gis brgyan pa dang //rgyu ma glad pa dbang po dang /bcas pa lha rnams dag la dbul//ru dra snying khrag dngas ma ste//khrag chen
78b5(tsha): mchod pa sbrel bar bya//de ltar mchod pa bya ba'o//de nas bsgrub pa rtsal bar bya//zhes gsungs so//rta mchog rol pa'i tan tra las//rta mchog rol pa'i tan tra las/rta mgrin bsgrub pa rdzas dang dkyil 'khor
78b6(tsha): bstan pa'i le'u ste/brgya rje gcig pa'o///de nas las kyi bzhung bstan te//de nas nyid kyi ting 'dzin ni//chos rnams stong pa'i rang bzhin bsgom//kun tu snang gi ting 'dzini//mthun
78b7(tsha): mangs ma yin khyab pa'i snying rje yin//de'i dran skyed snying rje mchog/snying rje lam du mchog pa yin//ngi las rgyu shar ting 'dzin hUM//sems kyi 'bras bu bstan pa yin//hUM las byung ba e shar bas//'byung ba'i gzhi ma
79a1(tsha): namakha' yin//hUM las yi ge yam shar bas//rten pa'i rlung zhes shar ba yin//hUM las yi ge ba ma shar nas//chu'i 'byung ba sa bon rten/hUM las yi ge su shar bas/sra ba sa'i brten du gyur//hUM las yi ge ram shar bas//smin cing rtas par byas pa yin//hUM las yi ge keng shar bas//
79a2(tsha): ring chen po byung ba yin/bul las ri rab gal pa yas byung /rag 'dra bar po ste/mkhas pa brtsegs pa yin/thumams pa yas rna mas dmar /nga kyil rimigs pa'i rten macan pa/bdu phrin po la sogs pa/nye zla 'dra rjegs pa 'tshul//gsang pa'i ma mas ni gyi la du'o//'ong snang gi la shar ba/dam sa
79a3(tsha): ma pa'i yi ge hUM ka de las 'og gi sngags brjod pas//rta mgrin rgyal por gyur pa yin//de la rigs pan sngags brjod pas//he'u'i rgyal por gyur pa yin//de ltar rang bzhin gsal bar bsgom//oM hU lu hu lu hUM phaT/de brjod rta mgrin rgyal por gyur//sAhA he ru ka kI la ya hU~M phaT/a mo ka shri/he ru ka hUM phaT/
79a4(tsha): 'dam a moka shri e'u ga hU~M pa Ta/karmA a moka hUM phaT/de kur ting 'dzin dkyil 'khor gas la bar bsgom ka rta mchog lob pa'i ska pas/nam mi ma du lani dang bcas pa bsgom pa'i le'u ste/brgyache gnyis pa'o///hru ngur khrong ma ltar 'ba' ba'i mazhal yas 'dir/chos dbyings dag pa/
79b1(tsha): mgrin rgyal po'i thugs dam bskongs mdzod//sha chen mkha' ldi shel sgong gzugs dang bcas//sman chen lha dang tshe dang byin se//rdo rje las kyi chu dang pany+tsa a mri ta ka sbyor dang sgrol gyi thugs dam mchog lags kyis//he ru bzhi'i thugs dam bskongs par mdzod ka/pad+ma zil tig tri dang k+ta kha/rdo rje las kyi
79b2(tsha): nga pany+tsa a mri ti ka/sbyor dang sgrol gyi thugs dam bskongs lags kyis//mkha' 'gro ma tshogs thugs dam bskongs par mdzod//pad+ma ban nga lcang lor bcas pa'i bdud rtsi 'di//dbang gi rgyal po khro bo rta mchog la//bla mar ngam rdzas bdud rtsi'i mchog/gnyisu med pa'i tshul gyis 'bul lags na//thugs
79b3(tsha): zhes nas dbang gis dngos grub gsol//'bar ba'i sbrul bya mngon par shar ba ni//rigs gnyis sprul pa'i khro bo bzhi rnams laka rnam dag bdud rtsi ma rdzas mnyes/mchod 'bul//thugs rje che dgongs dbang dang dngos grub gsol ka/ces sman mchod pa'o//yi ge 'bru gsum sku gsum thugs//byin
79b4(tsha): brlabs dam pa mdzad nas su//rtsa sku gsung phug su mdzad du gsol//bak+ka sid+d+hi aM/tsit+ta sid d+hi hUM /ka ya sid+hi i/hUM srid pa'i btso snying bzad par bya ba'i phyir //bdud rtsi chen po pany+tsa armi ta//bla med sman gyi mchod pa 'bul lags na//'khor ba'i sdug bsngal bzlog phyir bzhes su gsol//bla med mchog
79b5(tsha): gi mchod pa dam pa sbyar ba 'di//ye shes lnga'i 'od zer rnam par 'phro//'dod pa'i yon tan lnga yis rab brgyan nas//dam tshig gsang ba'i mchod par bzhes su gsol//zhes mchod pa dbul lo//de nas 'dod don smon lam btab//dngos grub slang dang phrin las bcol//nye bar bskur dang snyan pa bskyal
79b6(tsha): de nas las kyi bya ba bya//tshogs kyi mchod pa bya ba dang //bskang ba dang ni bshags pa dang ka las bcol ba dang gdeb par bya/oM hri ba mo sha ya ki la ya phaT//de ni dbang gis phur pas gang ba//swA hA ki la ya rbang //a mo ki la ya rbang /rang ka ta na ki la ya rbad//kar sha a mo ki la ya hUM phaT/
79b7(tsha): de ltar gsal bas gangs pa'o//de nas bro ste 'khor gsum brdung //de nas mchong ba dbul bar bya ste/bstod pa dang ni las bya'o//rta mgrin rol pa'i tan+da las/d+ha ma gri mi he ru bzhi dang bcas pa bsgrub pa'i le'u ste/brgya nyer gsum pa'o///de nas yang bstod pa 'di skad
80a1(tsha): ngo hUM dmar mag zla gam gdengs pa'i dkyil 'khor nas//dbang gi rgyal po pad+ma na'i rgyal/rta mgrin rgyal po drag pa 'dul ba'i lha/sku mdog dmar po me'i phung po 'dra/'bar ba'i phyag gis gdug pa'i phyag 'tshan 'dzin/ral gri sbrul zhag phong 'gri khu ga 'dzin/gdus/
80a2(tsha): pa'i zhabs kyis klu tshogs gdan du 'dings//na bza' 'bar ba sku'i rgyan du ston//glang chen ko rlon brjid chen sku'i chas//zhing lpags g.yang gzhi thabs la mkhas pas dag pa gsung gi chasaka/stag gi lpags pa dpa' bar 'gro ba thugs kyi chas//gdug gu sbrul na gzil gnon chas//rdo rje gshog
80a3(tsha): ba g.yas g.yon gdeng ba yis//thabs dang shes rab ldan te gdug pa zil gyis gnon//rdo rje rtse mo bya khyud 'bar bar klu gnyan sbrum bur thu//mche rtsigs rkan sgra drag pos bdud dpung zil gyis gnon//sprul pa mang po skar tshogs bkye bas pho nya mang po 'gug/dbu'i gtsug na rta mgo 'bar
80a4(tsha): /bas ngan song 'dub ba'i thabs las mkhas//lhang lhang rtser rtser lan gsum sgra sgrags pas ka mya ngan 'das pa'i mtshams na rtse re re ka/ngan song gnas rnams stongs zhing ye shes don la bskul//gsung rabs mnyes pas sgra'i sgrogs shing bde gshegs thugs dam bskul/'khor ba gtan
80a5(tsha): nas 'joms shing byang chub don la 'khod//snyan pa'i sgra rnams sgrags pas lha'i ma dbyingasu dgyes//ral pa dmar po btsigs pas bdud dpung zil gyis gnon //bdud bzhi bcom nas ye shes bzhi'i ngang la 'jog/ngan song gnas rnams stongs ste snying po byang chub lam la bkod//dbang gi
80a6(tsha): rgyal po ca skad mtsher ba khyod la bstod//sprul pa'i rtse ru khro bo rgyal po bani//rigs pazhi sprul pa khro bo bzhir sprul pa//phyogs bzhi dag nas gnas pa ste//gdug basbrul gyis sku sprul pa/smug nag pa 'bar ba'i thong 'phreng 'ching //nam za 'bar ba gsum bsnams pa/phyag na gdus
80a7(tsha): ba'i mtshon bsdams pa//g.yas pa dang po dgra sta lcag kyu dang //tha ma gdug pa'i phur ba 'dril//g.yon pa med dpung ba nang 'o//gdug pa 'dul phyir drag po'i las la brtson//sprul pa mang po yang dag las la brtson mi bzad pa'i sgra sgrogs shing //bdud dpung 'joms pa'i rkan lan rdzogs
80b1(tsha): srid pa yongs la phan pa'i bya re mdzad//sku'i brjid thabs 'bar ba'i sku//gsung ni gdug pa'i nga ro sgrogs//thugs ni dbang po'i rnage khrag phrin las mdzad//bshog pa'i bar bar lcags kyu 'bar bas bltams//sprul pa khro chung 'bar bas ba spu bu ga bltams//ye shes mkha' 'gro mang po
80b2(tsha): las la 'gyed//srid pa'i ri rab yang ni 'gram nas bsnyil//rgya mtsho chen po gzhung yang 'gegs par byed//me ri me dpung 'bar ba'i klong na bzhugs//dur khrod chas kyi sku la rgyal du byas//mche ba rnon po skye shi rtsad nas gcod//spyan mig bzlog pa tsam gyis khams gsum bran du 'khol//thugs
80b3(tsha): rje dbyings su 'dren cing 'khor ba bla meng sa la 'khod//rngam chen he ru bzhi la phyag 'tshal bstod//ces gsungaso//rta mgrin rol pa'i tan+t+ra las//rta mgrin dang 'khor he ru bzhi la bstod pa'i le'u ste/brgya nyer bzhi pa'o///de nas yang dbang gi rgyal pos//yang 'di skad ce/
80b4(tsha): bka' stsal to//dmu long la/longs spyod brgyad sbrel pa sum bcu rtsa gnyis dang //dmu khong las kyis 'khor ba brgyad bya ba yod de//long brgyad spel pa sum bcu rtsa gnyis ni//mkha' 'gro ma sum bcu rtsa gnyis sprul pa yin//dmun long las kyis 'khor ba brgyad bya ba ni//dbang gi
80b5(tsha): rgyal po'i thugs kyi sgro bsrungs brgyad yin te//'phags pa'i lam yan lag brgyad la gol nas//rtse gcig ting 'dzin ka po bsnyon pa'i ting 'dzin la gnas so//khro bo brgyad yod pa lta bu ma yin te//thub pa dmu long las/rnam par shes pa'i gzugs gang yang med do//lus
80b6(tsha): gal te bag chags pa las//phyag bzhin gdug pa'i mtshon cha thogs pa'o//mig med pa la gang yang rgyug pa la sto ba med pa'o//sna med pa las dri thams cad lus kyi tshor ba'o//lce med pa las drag po'i sgra khong nas smra'o//rna ba med pa las sgra thams cad khong nas thos pa'o//dmu
80b7(tsha): long lta bu de phrin las gang la yang thos pa med par bya'o//de lta bu de bsgrub par bya bar gsungs//dbus su rta mchog rgyal po de//le'u rgyad pa dmu long brgyad//lha dgu pa ru bsgrub par bya'o//dkyil 'khor thig bzhi btab pas le'u dgu/rdo mtshon rigs dang mthun par bya//sgo
81a1(tsha): @gcig pa ni shar du'o//de la snyen bsgrub phyir ltar bya//dang po sngon du 'gro ba'i las//rim pa brgyad par ldan par bya//de nas gzhung la 'jug pa ste//ting 'dzin rnam gsum rim bzhin bsgom//de nas rig pa'i hUM de las//'byung ba lnga po bskyed par bya//

81a4(tsha): kha ma le hUM/sngags de raM brjod pa yin//dmun long 'khor ba re re bskyed//le'u tshe brgyad la bkod pa'o//bcom ldan dab chen he ru ka/khro bo'i rgyal po hUM sgra can/che bas che ba bstod pa'i dbang phyug yang chen dpal//'bar ba lha'i lha la phyag 'tsha ltod//gdug pa 'dul phyir skyod pa'i dmu
81a5(tsha): long brgyad//srid pa dbang du bsdud pas rngam pas khro bo che//gdul dka' ma lus 'dul ba'i dmu long gya nom pa/dbang gis zhing khams chen po sbyang ba dang //bdud dang srin po klu rnams tshar gcad dang //dngos grub dam pa sgrub pa'i phyir//gsang ba'i dbang chen kun 'dul bka' khor/
81a6(tsha): bskyang ba dang //dbang drag las kyi phrin las bsgrub mdzad pa/dmu lo nga las kyi 'khor la phyag 'tshal bstod/de ni bskyed dang bstod pa'o//de nas spyan ni drang byas te//mi dmigs chos dbyings dag pa las//dbang chen pad+ma he ru bshegasu gsol//gdug pa ma lus 'dul ba dang //
81a7(tsha): dngos grub mchog rnams gsol ba'i phyir//'bar ba'i gdan la bzhugs su gsol//yul drug rten dag gnas brgyad nas//gdug pa 'dul phyir long ba'i tshogs sprul nas/dbang drag 'phrin las mdzad pa'i mchog/tshom bu 'od kyi gnas su gshegs su gsol/'bar ba'i zhing gi 'od
