81b2(tsha): 'dod chags tshul gyis 'dod chags 'joms mdzad pa//'dod chags rgyal po pad+ma he ru ka/dbang mchog gdug pa 'dul phyir dus las mi 'da' ba//pad+ma he ru ka la phyag 'tshal lo//khams gsum 'khor ba ru las bzlog pa'i phyir//gdug pa'i tshogs chen sku sprul pa/las kyi dmu
81b3(tsha): lo brgyad po'i tshul//'bar ba tshogs chen dag la phyag 'tshalo//srid pa'i gdul dka' ma lus 'dul ba dang //bka'i phrin las ma lus grub pa bya ba'i phyir/gsang ba'i mkha' 'gro'i tshogs la phyag 'tshalo//a ti pu ho//'byung ba dag pa'i mchod pa dang //ril mo sgra'i mchod pa
81b4(tsha): phyi nang gsang ba'i mchod pas mchod//dngos grub blangs la lhag ma sgreng ka smon lam gang ba dang phrin las bcol//chags nyams bshags la rig pa'i me tog dor ka hu lu 'dzab kyi dgongs pa bya//bgrangs tshang nas ni bstod dang 'phrin las bcol//'byor pa tshe Da kyi mchod
81b5(tsha): ba bya nga mtshan ma dgugs dmigs gang ba te bslab pa bya//lhag ma btang ste mkha' 'gro mchod//'bum lnga bshegs pa'i yi ge brgya pa gdon//de ltar byas la bsgrub pa po//gnan pa dang ni 'phang baM dang //bsregs pa'i las ni yang dag bya//mtha' bsdu ba'i lasu gsung
81b6(tsha): bskang dang bzlog pa bya ba ni/las gzhung srang la bya ba ste/drag po'i las gsum bcung la bya ba yin//zhes gsung so//rta mchog rol pa'i tAn+da las//dbang gi rgya la bo dbu long dang bcas pa'i las kyi rim pa'i le'u ste/brgya nyer drug pa'o//de nas dbang gi rgyal po pad+ma ru
81b7(tsha): gsungs pa//bcom ldan 'das dbang chen pad+me he ru ka'i zhabs brgyad kyi 'og na/khro bo bcu tham pa yod pa de//phyogs mtshams brgyad du khro bo brgyad bsnol gan brgyal lo//stobs po che dang dU kar//dbus su gan brgyal kha sgrub po//zhabs brgyad long bu man chad khong du nub nas non no//de nas
82a1(tsha): dbang gi rgyal po pad+ma he ru kas//khro bo brgyad 'bar ba'i mi bzad pa'i skur ston nas//'og dur khrod 'bar ba//steng du rdo rje 'bar ba'i gur//bar rin po che 'bar ba'i gzhal yas khang //dkyil du thod pa 'bar ba'i gzhal yas khang na//khro bo chen po brgyad gnas pa te//de yang bsgrub
82a2(tsha): par bya ba ste//dbang gi rgyal po kun 'dus te//khro bo bcu yis bskor ba bsgrub pa'o//dkyil 'khor gru bzhi lham pa la//dbus su zlum po zla gam bcas//tha ma dbus su 'khor lo'i rtsibs ni bcu pa bya//gru bzhi 'bur dang sgo dang bcas pa bri/las kyi rim pas de bzhin bya//gsung ngam sku ste/
82a3(tsha): phyag rgya thugs/'khor lo bcu la dgod pa'o//de la rdzas rnams de bzhin bzhag/rnam gsum rims kyis ting 'dzin sgom//'bras bu dang ni rgyu ru bshad//'byung ba rims bzhin bskyed pa'o//de las gzhal yas gsal bar bsgom/me 'bar ye shes 'bar ba'i gzhal yas khang
82a4(tsha): phyi rang nang med kun tu snang //'bar ba'i 'khor lo rtsibs brtu le/gru chang bar chad gru bzhi ldan//de ltar gsal ba'i ting 'dzin bsgom//de'i dkyil dulha rnamaso//oM hu lu hu lu hUM phaT/badz+ra kro d+ha hUM kA ra/badz+ra kro d+ha bi dza ya/badz+ra kro d+ha ni la tan tA/badz+ra kro d+ha ni kha tan tra/badz+ra/
82a5(tsha): kro d+ha ya man ta ka/badz+ra kr-i d+ha ar+ya a tsa la/badz+ra kro ta ha ya 'gri ba/badz+ra kro d+hi a pa ra tsi ta/badz+ra kro d+ha a mr-i ta kun d+ha li//badz+ra kro d+ha trai log kyang bi dza ya/badz+ra kro d+ha ma hA ba la hUM/'khor lo bcu la hUM de gong do//bcu ni rim par dgod pa'o//hUM hUM hU~M hU~M hU~M hU~M hU~M hU~M hU~M hU~M/dbus su hUM dmar/
82a6(tsha): la bkra ba yin par gsungs//hUM las sngags des brjod pa yis/'bar ba'i khro bor gyur pa'o//de ltar gzhal yas khang gi na na ni//ro de bzhugs pa'i gzugs dag pa bskyed pa dang /lha'i ti 'dzin gyi dkyil 'khor ro//zhes gsung so//rta mchog rol pa'i tan+da las//ting nge
82a7(tsha): ma nga' thubs brgyan bstan pa'i le'u sa brtse byer bdud pa'o// nas lharnams spyan dpa bya skyes rnam par martos chab byings dag sanas kyi dang chen rgyam 'i kun 'dus gsheg su gsolas gnyis meng rig 'bugs kyi gnas can nas
82b1(tsha): khro bo 'khor bcas gshegs su gsol//gnyis med rol pas chub pa'i dkyil 'khor nas//che bas che bar bstod pa'i dbang phyug yang che dpal ka 'bar ba'i lha'i lha la phyag 'tshalo//srid gsum gdug pa ma lus kun 'joms pa//gdug pa 'dul mdzad ye shes lha'i lha//stobs
82b2(tsha): kyi rim pa'i khro bo hUM sgra can//hUM mdzad khro bo bcu la phyag 'tshalo//theg pa dgu la gnas shing sa dgu rims kyis sbyong //pha rol phyin bcu rdzogs shing mtha' gnyis mi spong dang la gnas//sgo ba mgo rtsegs bzhi la phyag lo//de nas bstod pa'i rim pas mchod//smon lam gdab
82b3(tsha): nga 'ad don gsol//lha'i snyen pa grangasu skyel//gang 'dod dbang drag las dang sbyar//de las lha rnams dag la bstod//hUM khrag mthung 'bar ba'i skur bstan pa//dbu lnga phyag ni bcu drug ste//'bar ba'i zhabs ni brgyad du bsnams//gshogs pa g.yas g.yon gnyisu gdengs//bshog rtse
82b4(tsha): bya khyud 'bar ba'o//nyi ma zla bas 'bar bar brgyan//'bar ba'i phyag mtshan mang po bsnams//dbu gdengs sangs rgyas lnga'i tshem bu bcas//dbu lnga rta mgrin 'bar ba lnga//tshon gang 'od kyi 'bru dang bcas//dbang gi rgyal po'i sku la phyag 'tshal bstod//hUM khro bo chen po hUM chen 'bar//brjid
82b5(tsha): pa'i stabs dang 'debs pa'i stobs//mi zad gdug pa 'dul mdzad pa//khro chen hUM ka ra la bstod//hUM nyon mongs kun las rnam par rgyal//rgyal ba rgyal ba'i rtags mnyes pa//chos dbyings dag dang mnyam par sbyor//gdug 'dul rnam par rgyal la bstod //rdzu 'phrul dpal mnga'
82b6(tsha): brnyes cing //rtags kyi chos dbyings dbyug pa yis//nyon mongs dra ba 'joms mdzad pa/'bar ba dbyug sngon can la bstod//chos dbyings dag pa'i ngang nyid las//lhag pa'i ye shes skur bstan pas//log par lta ba'i gshin rje sgral/de bshed gshin rje'i gshed la stod//chos nyid mnyam pa'i
82b7(tsha): ngang nyid las//g.yo ba med pa mi g.yo mgon//de sbrul bdud dang srin po tshar gcod pa//khro mchog mi g.yo mgon la phyag 'tshal bstod//'dod chags gnas su dag byed pa/theg pa'i rta la rab rgyun nas//mchog tu klung dang gnyen po 'dul//rta mchog dpal la phyag 'tshal bstod//
83a1(tsha): dag pa'i don mchog mtshon pa yis//mnyam nyid ngang gis thams cad 'joms//de phyir gzhan gyis mi thub ste//gzhan gyis mi thub pa la phyag 'tshal bstod//chos dbyings dag pa'i bdud rtsi yis//nyon mongs ma lus gnasu bcom//'khor ba'i bdud la yis//
83a2(tsha): rtsis gtong ba//bdud rtsi 'khyil ba de la bstod//khamasu nyon mongs kun las rnam par rgyal//de'i don nyid mnor dbyings su bsdud byed pa//de'i don gyis dug gsum gnasu stongs par byed//khro chen khams sum rgyal la phyag 'tshal bstod//ye shes stobs dang ldan pa yis//
83a3(tsha): nyon mongs dra ba gcod byed pa//de las ma g.yos stobs po che//'joms byed stobs po che la phyag 'tshal bstod//ces gsung so//rta mchog rol pa'i tan tra las/lha la bstod pa bstan pa'i le'u ste/brgya nyer brgyad pa'o///de nas drag po gsang ba'i sbaT
83a4(tsha): gdab pa bstan//oM hri sti hU~M pa ya rbad phaT/gardza gardza hUM phaT/ha na ha na hUM phaT/d+ha ha d+ha ha hUM phaT/pa tsa pa tsa hUM phaT/ban da banA da hUM phaT/hu lu hu lu hUM phaT/stiSh+Tha stiSh+Tha hUM phaT/tsin d+ha tsin d+ha hUM phaT/bin+da bin+d+ha hUM phaT/s+pho ta s+pho ta hUM phaT/ces pas rim par gdab par bya ba yin//oM
83a5(tsha): badz+rI kro d+ha pa ra bing tran ga ga na sa ma ya phaT rbad/spyir dril ba gdab pa'o//gdab nas lhag ma bstab par bya//de'i lhag ma'i rjes shul la//yi ge nyi shu 'phro 'du las//khro bo bcu rnams spros pa'i bro brdung ba dang //yang sbrul khro chung mtshon gang gis//drag po'i las byed
83a6(tsha): pa'i blo brdung ba'o//de nas khro bo bcu'i gnad gzir bya ba yin//yi ge sngags kyi 'khor lor dgod//de ltar bsam pas las rnams rdzogs zhes gsungs//rta mchog rol pa'i tan tra las//sngags kyis gdab pa dang bro gnan pa'i le'u ste/brgya nyer dgu pa'o//
83a7(tsha): de nas dpal gyis bka' stsal pa//de nas rigs bsngos gsang ba yis//lhun gyis grub pa'i dkyil 'khor gyis//thams cad rgyas pa'i dkyil 'khor ste//bsgrub pa chen po byed pa dang //drag po ngal chen po byed pa dang //rigs bsdus gsang ba'i dkyil 'khor gsungs
83b1(tsha): dkyil 'khor gru bzhi lham pa la/dbus su pha brang zlum po bskor ka dkyil du zhig bani btab pas le'u dguka le'u dgu la pho brang dgu//dbus su pad+ma zla gam brgyad ka shar gyi le'u rtse dag la ni//rdo rje dbyings kyi dkyil 'khor ste//tshom bu lnga'i dkyil 'khor lo/ka/lho'i le'u
83b2(tsha): tshe dag la 'dul ba ste//dbus su zla gam tha ma 'khor lo brgyad//nub du rta mgrin dbang gis ste//zla gam kha sprod dag dang ldan//byang du khams sum rnam rgyal ste//zlum po pad+ma 'dab brgyad do//lho'i khe rtse dag la ni//ngan song sbyong ba'i dkyil 'khor ste/gru gsum zla gam
83b3(tsha): bzhis bskor ba'o//lho nub gru chad le'u dag la ni/hUM mdzad chen po'i dkyil 'khor ste//leru dgu dang ldan pa'o//nub byang le'u tshe dag la ni/dur khrod chen po'i dkyil 'khor ste/gru gsum pha ma bzlum po bzhi'o//byang shar zla gam sgo gcig pa//ye shes sems dpa
83b4(tsha): dkyil 'khor ro//mtha' ma gru bzhi'i sgo'i bskor//le'u re'i tha ma yang //gru bzhi sgo dang bcas pa'o//gru chang khyud ni kun la 'jug/de la lha rnams gsal ba ste//de ltar gsal bar bris sgoms nas//dbang gi byin brlabs chen po 'byung //dbang bskur dngos grub blang ba yang
83b5(tsha): de ltar byas na dngos grub thob//zhes gsungaso//rta mchog rol pa'i tan las/dbang gi rigs thams cad 'dus pa'i dkyil 'khor gyi le'u ste/brgya sum bcu pa'o////de nas bcom ldan 'das dbang gi rgyal pos bka' stsal pa//sems dpa' la sogs khyed rnams
83b6(tsha): nyon du byang chub 'bras bu thob 'dod pas//dge ba'i las su bstan pa ni//rtag tu mchod pa byed pa'i rten//mchod pa dang cing gus byas na//yid 'ong mdzes pa'i sa bzhi la//ba chu dang ni dri zhim pa'i mgo ma yeng //spos kyi chu'i chag chag gdab//mchod rten pad ma rtsegs
83b7(tsha): pa'i zin chen sna tshogs grub pa'i gzhal yas khang //pad+ma rigs kyi lha rnams bskyed pa rtag par bzhugs par bsgom//spyan drangs pa yang de la bstim/mchod pa rnams balnga po dang //rmad du byung ba gsum mchod nas//de nas snyen par sgra bsgrags cing //'gro ba
84a1(tsha): d+hU lnga'i 'khor los 'khor ba du//sdug bsngal 'khrul 'khrul 'khor ba zin pa'i 'gro gzigs nas//thugs rjes sdug bsngal ma lus 'joms mdzad pa//thugs rje'i bdag nyid khyod la phyag 'tshal stong//sems can smin cing sgrol bar mdzad pa'i phyir//chos
84a2(tsha): nyid de bzhin nyid la bzhugs gyur nas//chags pas ma gos pad+ma ltar dag pa'i//'dod chags dag pa'i bdag nyid khyod la bstod//sems can 'khor ba'i rgya mtshor nub pa la//thugs rje'i thabs dang shes rab gyur gzhag nas//zhi ba rin chen rgya mtsho'i gling la bskyed//kun gyis ded
84a3(tsha): dpon khyod la 'dud//sems can bkren pas chos kyis 'phongs gyur pa//ma rigs mun 'thibs nang du song ba rnams//'phags pa'i nor bdun dag gis tshim mdzad pa//ye shes sgron ma khyod la phyag 'tshal lo//sems can las kyi chu bos brngas pa rnams//dam chos bdud rtsi'i zam
84a4(tsha): pas sgrol mdzad cing //'phags lam yan lag brgyad la 'god mdzad pa'i//thugs rje'i bdag nyid khyod la phyag 'tshal bstod//mchod rten de rin po che sna tshogs las grub pa'i thugs rje dang rang bzhin gyis ye shes su bsam la/de'i bum pa'i nang du gnas pa'i skang po dang
84a5(tsha): mchod rten chen po de las/'od zer du ma grangs med cing bgrangs med pas//'khrul pa'i sems can thams cad kyi//las dang nyon mongs sgrib pa'i//tshogs rnams thams cad sbyad byas nas//de bzhin gshegs pa'i thams cad thugs//ye shes 'od zer tshul gyis bsdus bya/
84a6(tsha): nas nga mchod rten de la thim pa yis//mchod rten chen po de nyid kyis ni//de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi//chos ska long skra sbrul sgo'o//ngo bo nyid du bsgoms byas la//de la mchod pa kun mchod la//rten dang 'brel pa'i snying po bzlas//rigs kyi stod pa kun byas la//de ma
84a7(tsha): theg nas tshig 'di brjod//bcom ldan 'das te de bzhin gshegs//dgra bcom yang dag rdzogs pa'i//rigs pa zhabasu ldan pa'i bde bar gshegs pa rnams//skyes bu 'dul dang kha lo bsgyur//lha dang mi'i ston pa'i mchog/ngo mtshar cho 'phrul zung ldan pa'i//sang+yas sku
84b1(tsha): /gsum lhun grub ding 'dir rnams la dgongs su gsol//bdag gis sems can thams cad dbugs dbyung gis//bdud dang mu stegs la sogs 'pham par bgyi ba dang //sems can las dang nyon mongs rtog pa gsal bar bgyi//de las bdag cag rnams la ni//skyeb cing
84b2(tsha): gnang ba mdzad du gsol//de brjod la ni smon lam gdab//ces gsungs so//rta mchog rol pa'i tan+ta las//mchod pa'i rten gyi le'u ste/brgya so gcig pa'o///de nas rab tu gnas par bya//thugs rjer ldan pa'i ston pa yis//'gro ba bde la dgod po'i phyir//
84b3(tsha): sku gdung dang ni ring srel dang //mchod pa'i rten ni bzhengs par gsungs/sa dang pha rol phyin pa dang //smin pa dang ni grol ba'i phyir//mchod rten chen po bzhi yang bzhengs//lhag pa dang ni gzung 'jug dang //sku dang ye shes thob pa'i phyir//mchod rten bzhi yang bzhengs pa
84b4(tsha): bya sgrib gnyis byang nas tshogs gnyis rdzogs//de'i phyir yang bzhengs par bya//las dang nyon mongs sgrib sbyang nas//'bras bu byang chub thob pa'i phyir//de yang gzugs kyu gzhengs par bya//bdud kyi b+ha ras dang gnod sbyin mdun su dang //gdugu pa klu gtan cu
84b5(tsha): pa'i rgyun la medo//thugs rje zhi ba'i phyir yang dgod par bya//thugs rjes de la mchod rten bzhengs pa ni//rgyu'i mchod pa'i rten dang 'bru'i mchod rten no//rgyu dang 'bras bu 'brel par snang bas mchod rten rang bzhin yin//shar du zhi ba'i rdo rje rigs kyi mchod rten bzhengs pa
84b6(tsha): byang mchod rten zlum po rdo rje rtsegs pa'i kacan//'khor lo dang ldan rin chen lha smras nas//'khor lo dang ni gdun chen dang //bya ru dang ni skyabs mdzad pa//dar 'phyangs dang ni me tog 'phreng /mi 'gyur lcags phrag bzhi pa'o//rim chen rigs kyi zhing khams su//rin chem/
84b7(tsha): rten sngon po las/gru bzhi pa la rin chen rtsegs//ka ba rin po che dang ldam/sgo ni rin chen nor bu'i dbyibs/nub phyogs pad+ma rtsegaso pa'i mchod rten la/pad+ma ma stong dang ldan par bya//zhabs ni zla gam tshul du gnas//dzaM ka ba rin chen sgo//dmar po brgyan dang ldan pa'o//byang phyogs
85a1(tsha): las kyi rigs kyi mcho rten te//sdang khu gru gsum 'thur da bcas//rgya gram brtsegs pa'i tshul ngug nas//rin chen sna lnga'i brgyan dang ka bas mtsham/dbu su gser gyi mchod rten bkra shis sgo mangs la//'khol ba 'dra ba rdo rjes spras/snyan pa'i sgra sgrogs/
85a2(tsha): dril bu dang //bya rung nga ni ye shes dang //'kho ro la thugs kyi mchod rten te//'khol po mi 'gyur chos sgra skor/dar 'byangs g.yas dang g.yon du ste//g.yas pa chos skur 'gyur ba med//g.yon pa shes bya thogs pa med//de ltar mchod pa'i rten bzhengs pa//de la mtshad
85a3(tsha): rnams kyis ni//shar phyogs byams pa brtsegs pa'i mchod rten dang //snying rje rin chen rdzogs pa'i mchod rten dang //dga' ba pad+ma brtsegs pa'i mchod rten dang //btang snyoms don kun grub pa'i mchod rten ni//phyi'i tshad med mchod rten yin//mchod rten rgyu'i chos nyid
85a4(tsha): yin te//byin gyis brlabs dang ldan par bya/sa gzhi chos kyi dbyingasu brlab/khri layang dge ba bcu dang ldan par byin gyis brlabs//bang rim bzhi ni dran par bzhag pa bzhi ru bsgom//rdzu 'phrul gyi rkang pa bzhi//yang dag par spong ba bzhi//dbang po lnga'o//bum cen
85a5(tsha): stobs lnga dang //bum pa ni byang chub kyi yan lag bdun/bre ni 'phags pa'i lam yan lag brgyad dang //de la sogs par skyed pa'o//yid kyi mchod rten ting 'dzin sku//na mkha'i dbyings dang rgya mtsho 'dzings dag la/byang chub sems kyi mchod rten bris//de la ting 'dzin
85a6(tsha): yid kyis mchod pas mchod//yang ni mchod pa'i rten bshad pa//khri 'phangs yis bzhi ru skye//gyen du khri 'phangs rnam thar gsum//chags med pad+ma'i bdan stengs su//bum pa zlum po chos kyi rtse//gser gyi bre phul 'od zer 'phro//srog shing drang srong bla na med//dag pa
85a7(tsha): rnam lnga'i mchod pas mchod//rang byung ba'i mchod rten brgyad la//khyams ldan gter gyi rten dang //thar legs rdzogs pa'i mchod rten dang //sprul pa'i mchod rten dam pa dang //yid bzhin lhun gyis grub pa'i mchod rten dang /dpe med bsam las 'das pa'i mnad
85b1(tsha): rten dang wa yon tan rtsegs pa'i mchod rten dang //rmad grags chos nyid mchod rten dang //khams sum bde rgyas sgron med mchod rten no//de ni rang byung mchod rten brgyad //sprul pa'i mchod rten spang ni bshad//grangs dang dpag las 'das pa'o//rnam par thar pa'i zhing
85b2(tsha): khams na ka sku'i mchod rten bsam las 'das//ting 'dzin brten pa'i mchod pas mchod//rnam par dag pa'i zhing khams na//shes rab spyan dang rdzu 'phrul snyan//sku'i sangs rgyas kun gyis bskor//rnam par dag pa'i zhing khams na//shes rab spyan dang rdzu 'phrul snyan
85b3(tsha): sku'i sangs rgyas kun gyis bskor//rnam par dag pa'i zhing khams na//gsung gi mchod rten bsam las 'das//gsung gi mchod rten thams cad kyang //tshangs pa'i sgra skad phun sum tshogs//'gro ba drug la gsal mdzad pa//sgra'i dbyangs dang sde yis mchod//rnam

85b5(tsha): chu' bstong nga 'dod da gsas pa'o//zhes bsrung so//ma chog ros pa'i rnams/mtshar rkyen bye ba dang ngo skrun pa'i 'du ste brgya mo gnyis pa'o//de nas yang ni las gsungs pa/mchong rkyen gzhal yos bskyed pa dang /lharnams spyod pa bdun
85b6(tsha): rdo rje rigs kyi mchod rten du//khog par gzhal yas bsgom pa ste//tshom bu lnga'i gzhal yas la//rin chen dra bas brgyan pa dang //rdo rje ka bas mtshan pa'o//ka ba gdung dang bre phul re mig brgya na//'khor lo rin chen 'od 'bar ba'o//dbus su rdo rje sems dpa'
85b7(tsha): yin tha ma rigs pazhi sangs rgyas//de la rang rang rigs kyi sems dpa' sems ma sgo bas bskor ba'o//de la mchod dang las bya'o//rigs dang mthun pa'i mchod pa'o//rin chen rigs kyi mchod rten du/ka gdung bre phul re mig de bzhin te/tshom bu lnga'i dkyib
86a1(tsha): @'khor la//dbus su rin chen 'byung ldan la//rigs bzhis sangs rgyas bskor ba'o//sems dpa' sems ma 'khor dang bcas//de la rigs mthun mchod pas mchod//pad ma rtsegs pa'i mchod rten la//tshom bu lnga'i dkyil 'khor yin//dbus su
86a2(tsha): /snang ba mtha' yas la//phyogs bzhi rigs bzhi sangs rgyas yin//rang rang sems dpas bskor ba yin//byang phyogs las kyi rigs kyi mchod rten du//tshom bu lnga'i dkyil 'khor la//dbusu don yod grub pa yin//mtha' ma rigs bzhi sangs rgyas sems dpar bcas
86a3(tsha): de ltar rigs kyi mchod pas mchod//sngags dang phyag rgya gong bzhin no//sku'i mchod rten chen po ni//dbus su rnam par snang mdzad la//sems dpa' sems mas bskor ba'o//gsung gi mchod rten snang ba mtha' la//sems dpa' sems mas bskor ba'o//thugs
86a4(tsha): kyi mchod rten la//rdo rje sems dpa' la sems dang sems mas bskor ba'o//de la mchod dang 'dod don gsol//ces gsung so//rta mchog rol pa'i tan+t+ra las//mchod rten lha ma skyed pa'i le'u ste/brgya so gsum pa'o///de nas tshig bshad
86a5(tsha): bka' stsal paka gnyis med dag pa'i zhing khams nas//rang byung mchod rten chen po brgyad//'gro ba'i don du spyan drangs te//gzugs brnyan mchod rten dam pa la//byin gyis brlob cing gshegasu gsol//gsal dag mdag pa'i gnas mchog nas//
86a6(tsha): sprul pa'i mchod rten brgyad po yang //'gro ba'i don du spyan drangs te//gzugs brnyan mchod rten dam pa la//byin gyis brlob cing gshegasu gsol//rnam par dag pa'i zhing khams na//gsung gi mchod rten bsam las 'das//sna dang dbyangs dang sde'i
86a7(tsha): mkhyen pa'i ye shes gsal ba yis//ma rig mun pa kun sel ba//dmigs pa med pa'i ting 'dzin sgom//'gro ba'i don du spyan drangs te//gzugs brnyan mchod rten dam pa la//byin gyis brlobs shing gshegs su gsol//rnam par dag pa'i zhing khams nas///
86b1(tsha): /thugs kyi mchod rten bsam las 'das//mkhyen pa'i ye shes gsal ba yis//ma rigs mun pa sel mdzad pa//dmigs pa med pa'i ting 'dzin bsgom//'gro ba'i don du spyan drangs ste//gzugs brnyan mchod rten dam pa la//byin gyis brlobs shing gshegasu gsol/
86b2(tsha): yid kyi mchod rten ting 'dzin sku kan mkha'i dbyings dang rgya mtsho'i 'jings//byang chub sems kyi mchod rten bris//ma lus bzhugs pa thams cad kyang //ting 'dzin gsal ba'i byin brlabs kyis//'gro ba'i don la gshegs nas kyang //gzugs rnyan mchod rten dam pa la//
86b3(tsha): sbyin gyis brlobs cing gshegasu gsol//rigs kyi mchod rten chen po lnga//radu byung rten pa'i gnas yin te/'gro ba'i don du spyan drangs nas//gzugs brnyan mchod rten dam pa la//byin gyis brlob cing gsheg su gsol//yang dag don du dag mod kyang //'gro ba rnaMs
86b4(tsha): na slad du//phyag dang zhabs gnyis bsil nas kyang //rdo rje'i gdan la bzhugasu gsol/oM a ha ra a ha ra sarba bid pu tse de ga ga na sa ma ye swA hA/d+hu Sh+pepus pa/a lo ke/gan d+he/de ltar mchod dang bstod pa bya//sngags kyi rim pazlas par bya//phyag rgya yang ni de
86b5(tsha): babazhin gshegs/ces gsungaso//rta mchog rol pa'i tan+ta las//mchod pa'i rten spyan drangs pa'i le'u ste/brgya so bzhi pa'o///de nas sngags 'thon pa ste//na ma sa man ta bud d+ha nan//garba d+ha ra ma d+hardzu swA hA//sa gzhi chos kyi byin gyis brlabs pa'i
86b6(tsha): sngagaso//lag pa thal mo khong stong sbyar//sngags dang phyag rgya de'i byin gyis brlobs pa'o//lag pa thal mo khong stong sbyar//sor mo rnams ni phyir skyed de//pad+ma'i phyag rgya 'dra bar bya//na ma sa man ta shu nyan ta nan shu nyan na swA hA/rmad khri byin gyis brlabs pa'o//
86b7(tsha): lag pa thal mo sbyar ba phyir bskyed de//the'u chung gnyis ni nang du bkug/the'u bong gnyis kyi rtse mo ru //'dzug mo gnyis ni bkug la sbyar//na ma sa man ta 'a bu ta nan ta tha ga ta smi te swA hA hri ba A bud dag ga sa ma ya swA hA/dran pa nye bar bzhag pa'i phyag rgya yin/
87a1(tsha): galag pa rnam ma khong spyong sgyu 'dzu mo gnyis bskyed la tshigs ni gsum par dkrugs thams man ta 'byamams ti tus can twA dz+ra tatri shung de swAhA/de bzhin gshegs paso mo yang dag par rig pa'i sngags dang byas kyiste/yang /
87a2(tsha): par spong ba bzhin dang //rdzu 'phrul gyi rkang pa bzhi dang //dbang po lnga'i rang bzhin no//bum rten stobs lnga dang //bum pa'i yan lag bdun dang //'khor lo de bzhin gshegs pa'i stobs bcu dang //de bzhin gshegs pa'i thun mongs ma yin pa'i dran pa nye bar bzhag pa bzhi
87a3(tsha): dang la sogs pa 'bras bu'i chos nyid dbyingasu byin gyis brlabs pa'i sngags dang phyag rgya ni//stobs bcu'i sngags dang phyag rgyar 'dus ste//lag pa gnyis gcig du thal mo khong stong du sbyar te//mthe 'ong gnyis dang the'u chung gnyis nang du bcug la//gcig dang sbrang la 'di
87a4(tsha): ni stobs bcu'i phyag rgya ste//na ma se man ta bud ta nan sha pa la ka da ra sa hUM sa dza swA hA//tog dkar byin gyis brlabs pa ni//lag gnyis khu tshur bcangs la//gong mo gnyis bsgreng te tshigs dbu mar dgug/'phe 'ong gnyis the chung gnyis so sor bsgreng //rdo rje rtse lnga par bya//
87a5(tsha): la can ma sa man ta d+ha ra ma ka ya swA hA/dar dbyang mnyam pa nyid du byin gyis brlabs pa ni//lag pa gnyis thal mo khong stong sbyar//'dzug mo gnyis bkug 'the 'od nang du gzung //srin la gnyis kyang mthe 'ong bar 'dzug mo bteg pa'i gzungs/na ma sa man ta 'bu ta nan/sa
87a6(tsha): d+harma sa man+ta pra ba ta thA ga ta ma nu ga te swA hA/chos thaMd mnyam pa nyid du rtogs par byed pa'i sngags dang phyag rgya'o//de ltar gsal bar byas pa dang /dbang bskur spyan drangs zhal bso dang /lha'i spyan dbye ba'o//bskor ba bya dang sgra snyan bsgrags/dar gyi lda ldi 'chang phur gdag/
87a7(tsha): ces bsung so//rta mtshag rob pa'i tan tha las/sngags dang phyag rgya'i le'u ste ba rgyas bzhi pa'o//de nas yang phyag na rdo rje rang gis spyani las langs te/dbang chen 'dus pa'i rgyal po la 'di skad ces gsol to//seMn tshe'i dus byas nas/ngan song gnasu
87b1(tsha): /'khyams pa la//de la drad+hU ba ji ltar lags//bac+doMs kyis bsha du gsol//ces gsol to//dbang chen kun 'dus bka' stsal pa/sems can las kyi 'khyams pa de//'khor ba ngan song gnas nas drang pa na//thabs dang shes rab ldan pas drang //nyon cig rdo rje sadar
87b2(tsha): /cheHse naM tshe'i 'dus byas ste/ngan song 'khor ba'i gting na ni//rtogs pa'i dra bas bcings nas 'khor ba'i snying zin de//tshul bzhin chos nyid ngang du bsgral dgos pas//de nas sems can byin gyis brlabs te/gnyen po rang las byung ba yin//mig la yi ge ram bsgoms
87b3(tsha): pas/yis me'i dkyil 'khor gyur//yis me dang thugs rje byang chub sems kyis mes/se ni mtshan ma'i bag chaDa sreg par bya/sna las rlung gis dkyor gyur/bdag gi ti nge 'dzin yis rlung /skye med chos kyi rlung gi ni//bag chags de dag gtor bar bsgom//lce stengs khang nas
87b4(tsha): chu'i dkyil 'khor byung //ting 'dzin ye shes chu bo dang /byang chub sems kyi chu bo yis//bag cha Da de dag bkrus par bsgo//de nas brum zhes rab brjod pas//sems can phung po rin po che//gzhal yas khang du gyur pa ste//gzhal yas khang ni de nang du/ti hUM zhes ni brjod pa
87b5(tsha): yis na sems snying po ti'i dud ka da/thugs rje ta'i dud kas sbyangs//dud kas ting 'dzin yi ge hUM du gyur//de las dpa' bo 'bru lnga byung las dgod//spyi bo'i gtsug du oM yin te//lce la yi ge oM gyis mtshan//snying kar hUM ni dgod pa ste//lte bar yi swa yi no///
87b6(tsha): rkang pa'i yi ge ha yang bsgom//ha sa ha/mas bsdus pa yon no//swA hUM hUM hUM/oM oM/tshig bshing 'di yis bag cha Da sbyang //oM las ye shes na mkha' 'gyur/khyod kyis bag chags des byang ngo //aM las yis chu sbyar bas/khyod kyi bag chags des bkru'o
87b7(tsha): hUM las yis byung bas//khyod kyi lnga phung nges bstan no//sA las yis mi byung bas//khyod kyi bag chaT sdug bsngal nges bsreg go/ha las ye shes rlung byung bas//khyod kyi sgrib pa des gsal lo//rkang mthil yi ge haM bsgoms pas//don yod grub pa yang
88a1(tsha): @yum bskyed//phyag rgya chen po bzhi dang ldan//phyag rgya chen po'i byin brlabs kyis//phrag dog rigs kyi sgo gcad do//mi rnams kun gyis gnas//stong so//las kyi rigs kyi thugs ma bskongs//don yod gnasu sbar na phaT/lte bar yi ge swa bsgoms pas//
88a2(tsha): rin chen 'byung ldan yab yum bskyad//phyag rgya chen po bzhi dang ldan//phyag rgya chen po'i byin brlabs kyis//nga rgyal rigs kyi sgo bcad pas//lha ma yin gyis gnas kyang stongs//rin chen rigs kyi thugs dam bskongs//'byung ldan gnas su sa pha ra na phaT//snying khar yi ge hUM
88a3(tsha): /bsgoms pas//rdo rje sems dpa' yab yum bskyed//phyag rgya chen po bzhi dang ldan//phyag rgya chen po byin brlabs kyis//zhe sdang rigs kyi sgo bcad nas//dmyal ba rnams kyi gnas stong so//rdo rje rigs kyi thugs dam skongs//sems dpa'i gnas su s+pha ra na phaT/lce stengs yi ge aM bsgos
88a4(tsha): pas rba snang ba mtha' yas yab yum bskyed/phyag rgya chen po bzhi dang ldan//phyag rgya chen po'i byin brlabs kyis/'dod chags rigs kyi sgo gcad pas//yi dgas rnams kyis gnas stongs so//pad+ma rigs kyi thugs dam bskongs/mtha' yas gnasu s+pha ran phaT/spyi bor yi
88a5(tsha): bsgoms pas//rnam par snang mdzad yab yum skyed//phyag rgya chen po bzhi dang ldam/phyag rgya chen po'i byin brlabs kyis//gti mug rigs kyi sgo bcad pas//byol song rnams kyi gnas kyang stongs//de bzhin gshegs pa'i thugs dam bskongs/snang mdzad gnas su s+pha ra na 'aT//
88a6(tsha): de skad brjod pa'i sgo nas ni/mtshon gyi gru bu bsgongs pa bzhin//chos kyi sku ru gyur pa'o//de nas phung po sbyang ba dang //rnam par shes pa'i tshogs brgyad sbyang ba'i//don gyi 'di rnams brjod par gsungs/sems can chen po khyod nyon cig/khyod kyi snga phung yan lag ni//gzugs kyi
88a7(tsha): phung po snang mdzad do//tshor ba rin chen 'byung ldan te/'du shes snang ba mthe' yas yin/'du byed don yod grub pa yin//rnam shes rdo rje sems dpa' yin/phung po lnga yang mngon sangs rgyas//snyigs ma lnga yang bde ldan gnas/shi ba bya ba ming yang med/mi 'gyur
88b1(tsha): bstan pas gser bzhin no//sems can chen po khyod nyon cig khyod kyi gzugs kyi yan lag la//'byung ba chen po lnga gnas ste//khyod kyi shi na sa yin te//sa ni yum zhes sangs rgyas spyan/khyod kyi drod ni me yin ste/me ni yum zhes gos dkar mo//khyod kyi khrag ni chu yin te//
88b2(tsha): tshul ni yum zhes ma yin te/tshun yum zhes ma ma ki/khyod kyi dbugs ni rlung yin te//rlung ni yum zhes dam tshigs sgrol khyod kyi sems ni na mkha' yin//na mkha' kun tu bzang po min//'byung ba lnga yang mngon sangs rgyas/snyigs ma lnga yang ba ldan gnas//shi ga/
88b3(tsha): bya ba'i ming yang med//mi 'gyur bstan pas gser bzhin no//sems can chen po khyod nyon cig/khyod kyi rnam shes tshogs brgyad la//byang chub sems dpa' brgyad dgod do//rna ba phyag na rdo rje la/mig ni sa'i snying po yin/sna ni na mkha'i snying po yin//lce ni spyan
88b4(tsha): ras gzigs dbang ste//mgrin pa 'jam dpal gzhon nu yin//nyon mongs yid ni sgrib sel yin//rnams sems yid ni byams pa yin/kun gzhi kun tu bzang po yin//de'i yul brgyad lha mo brgyad//rnam shes brgyad kyang mngon sangs rgyas/shi ba bya ba maung yang
88b5(tsha): med mi 'gyur stan pas gser bzhin no//sems can chen po khyod nyon cig/khyod kyi yan lag sgo bzhi ni//khro bo bzhi'i bsgoms pa'o//lag pa g.yas pa stobs po che//lag pa g.yon pa gshin rje'i gshed//rkang pa g.yas pa rta mchog dpal//rkang pa g.yon
88b6(tsha): ba bdud rtsi 'khyal//de'i yum bzhi khro mo bzhi//mi 'pham ma dang khro gnyer ma//ral pa cig pa khro mo gce yan lag bzhi yang sngon sangs rgyas//shi ba bya ba ming yang med//mi 'gyur bstan pas gser bzhin no//de nas spyi yi skye 'gag dang //chos nyid rnam par
88b7(tsha): dag pa'i don gyi phyir//sems can thams cad bsang ba'i rgyu//ma skyes phyir yang gsang ba'o//sems can thad za ba'i rgyu//gdong nas snying po byang chub lung /sen bde gtub pa'i rgyu/skye mched byang chub semasu bsdu/sems can bde za ba'i rgyu//gnyis med byang chub
89a1(tsha): sas su brgyas/rbi ta sprugs nas dgu gzhibs/byang chub sems kyi don dang ldan/ra na pa'i rgyu ma thag yin te/tongs 'khor ba'i bag ma sbyang par// tsasamanam gsanams/ram nam skye chags pa rgyum thag yin te/'khor ba drab ma smras/
89a2(tsha): bsreg par bya'o//rak+ta chags pa'i rgyu ni khrag yin te/sdug bsngal rgya mtsho ma lus bskam par bya'o//tsit+ta'i mkhor te kha phye ste/snang gsal sdong bu gcig bsam mo//a kar lta bu'i byang chub sems/lus kyi sgrom bur sems gnas pa/dza hUM baM ho thugs khar thim/rtse mo
89a3(tsha): byung rgyal ba chen po//ljon shing dmar po thugs kar sa le gshegs//nyi ma stongs zhes che snyam ste//de bas gzim dang 'dus pa'i thig le che//rtag pa chen po dag la gang gnas pa//rtogs pa chen po longs spyod rdzogs pa'i sku/thig le chen po kun nas pad gnas pa'o//mkhas
89a4(tsha): khas rtsal kyang rnyed par ga la 'gyur//mi 'gyur yum gyi gzhal yas su//skal dang ldan pa khyod nyid ni//rgyal ba'i sras su bskyed par bya'o//pha rol phyin pa bcu rdzogs shes rab dbyings kyi gzhu/dam tshig snying rje 'brel pa'i gzhul bor zhing //dam tshig brgyan gyis mchog ma
89a5(tsha): rin chen gser//ka la bing ka li'i rgyu'i phreng la//mtha' gnyis bral ba'i ye shes gnyug ma la//thabs dang shes rab 'brel pa'i thabs la bsres//nyon mongs dug gsum nad kyis gdung ba'i chu phyung la//ye shes mkha' 'gro bzhi bskyon//rno dpal ting 'dzin mde'u yin
89a6(tsha): te/sku gsung thugs kyi sprul pa sprir gsum gyis/nga dang bdag du 'dzin pa'i rtog pa bcom/sku las skyed pa'i rgyal ba'i sras su bsgom/mi 'gyur yum gyi mkha' dbyings rnam dag pas//rgyal ba'i sras su ma lus skyed par byora'o//rgyal ba dgongs pa kun 'dus /
89a7(tsha): dbang yang de nyid nyid rdzogs//'gro don thabs kyi bskul ba'i mchog/'gro ba'i don la de bzhin gshegs/yang bzhu yang brtul gser dpe bzhin//bzhu brtul ting 'dzin rtsa la yang sbyar//de la nyes pa gos mi 'gyur//chos nyid la yang chos nyid rol//dkyir la
89b1(tsha): yang rgyal 'khor 'im/zhes gsung so//rta mchog rob par rgyud las/nyon mongs 'khor ba'i gnas nas drang zhing don la dgod pa'i leru ste/brgya so lnga pa'o///brjes dbang chen kun 'dus la//phyag na rdo rjes yang gsol pa/e ma'o dbang chen kun 'dus pa/
89b2(tsha): yi gtso//bdag la log par bdag du 'dzin cing lta//che dag mnan nam dang zhugs na//las kyi rim pa ji ltar lags//kun 'dus mchog gis bsha du gsol//ces gsungaso//dbang gi rgyal po des//gsol ba'i don thugasu chud nas//pad+ma'i rgyal po he ru kas//gzhir/
89b3(tsha): thabs chen po nub du mdzad//ye nas rgyu nyid dag pa la//nga dang bdag tu rtogs pa yis//khams gsum 'khor ba'i rgyu ru 'gyur//khams gsum de dag bsgral ba'i zhing //dbang du bsdu ba'i las mdzad kyis/sangs rgyas kun gyis byin gyis brlobs/khams gsum/
89b4(tsha): ma lus sgral ba'i zhing //rten chen po bya ba'i phyir//bde gshegs mang po kun 'dus shing //byams dang snying rje ldan pa yis//nyon mongs 'khor ba'i rgya bral ste//bdud dang gshin rje 'khor bcas kun//gnyis med rol pa'i phyag rgya yis//dus la bab po sa ma yA/
89b5(tsha): rnal 'byor dbang phyug chen po yis//rtse rgyal bstan pa'i gnas chen 'dir//bo de tsi ta'i dbyings nyid du//khams gsum 'khor ba kun bsdu bo//oM ma hA de ba ma hA ka ru tra//a gu sha dza/de ltar bkug pa'i ma btang de//dam bdar legs pa nyes par brjod//phar
89b6(tsha): pa'i la mo byed dkyil 'khor 'dir//brod pa byed pa 'ongs pa ltar//sbrul dang ldan pa rigs kyi bu//ding 'dir 'ong ba legs te 'ong //oM badz+ra a gu he tri sha sa ma ya snom//de yis phyag rgya brda' yang bstan//shar phyogs lam sgo'i 'jig rten na/yang dag thar lam gcod
89b7(tsha): pa yis//'byung po gtum po nga rgyal can//khyod ni sgrib pa kun la sgrib/yang dag shes rab ye shes kyi//rtsa khungs rtags bcang bdag las byung //bdog 'dzin bdag du rtogs pa ni//bdog med po ba'i thobs bzhom/bdag po babs stobs brtson 'grus kyis//gnas 'a
90a1(tsha): ba ri dang dpon byad//de nyid bdag rtogs yin pa la/bdag med stong pa'i stobs kyis gnom/ad man nyi ma hA ga shu nyan ta badz+ra mud ka a go ta ya hU~M phaT/sarba ba gar he duka pra be sha ma tang ka ru hUM phaT/kha T+wAM la ruHhU~M phaT/oM tra ba tri mu tsha ba tsin d+ha tsin d+ha d+hU ShaT/
90a2(tsha): sarba su ka/sarba karma/sarba kle sha tsi ni d+ha tsin d+ha hUM phaT/sarba ba ra tsi tran//khad ga tsin d+ha tsin d+ha hUM//dreg pa can tha daM dus gcig de'i sgra yis 'dus por bzhi//de yis ma trang bgyel ba yin//chos chen gyis ni sgo sgos te//dbang chen stobs kyang che ba'o//bdag stong mtshon cha/
90a3(tsha): rnon po yis//gdab pa dag kyang de nas thabs//rgyal ba'i sras rnams dngos grub rnam par 'jigs//srid pa ji bzhin ye nas stong pa yin//'phags pa thams cad bdag la gson//'byung po mang po kun nyon cig/rdo rje rnon po 'jam dpal gyis//shes rab dang ni snying rje dang //
90a4(tsha): thabs la mkhas pa'i byin brlabs kyis//'gro ba thad sbyang bar mdzad//'tsho ba'i bdag po tshe dang srog/gson po dag kyang bar gcod pa'i//bdud dang gshin rje 'khor bcas la//gzhon zhing bsad pa'i gnyen po ni//'jam dpal sprul pa'i 'khros pa'i mchog/sri gsum thar
90a5(tsha): byed rngam pa res//kha tr-ing long dbyug mi zad pas//gshin rje bcom nas gsad par bya//ro yang 'dzam bu'i gling du bor//sa gzhi dur khrod brgyad po yis//bdag po dregs pa bsnol ba yis//ro yi nyal thabs rgan rgyal te/migo ni lho nub 'tshams su bstan/rkang pa
90a6(tsha): bya tra sha thug pa med//de yang ma phrog gnyis snol po la/s+ya gi dang n-i gang phyo/laga' g.yon mo li khrig ma la/khyi gnyis dang ni bya nod de//bal ba'i mdog la lus bsgreng ba'o//oM shriH'dubye drugamacwa 'ardzas ma su sbyin 'am ya yu tra pra tshan cam ca
90a7(tsha): na hUM phaT/mi g.yo phyag rgya chen po'i ngang //mtshon pa'i bar du tshe dang srog/bang bar gshegs pa'i skyabasu gsol//'chi ba'i sdug bsngal 'dir 'byung ngo //ma trang chen po de bred nas/shi bas thon pa'i tshig du bstan//pad+ma'i rgyal po he ru kas/'di skad thugs las
90b1(tsha): thon pa'o//skye ba med pa'i rkyen gyis 'chi ba yin//sdig pa'i rkyen gyis bcos pas 'chi//bdag dang dngos pos lta bas 'dzin//nga'i bstan pa 'dri la bzung ka/'on kyang 'chi ba'i sdug bsngal bral//byang chub mchog tu sems skyed cing ka sa ya ke sha rtsi t+ta dz+wa la hUM phaT/
90b2(tsha): de yis zil gyis gnan par bya//de nas yang ni 'di skad de//gnas kyi sgo 'di su yis gcad//sems kyi bdag pos gcad dam tsi//gzung 'dzin rka lag bsnol ba yis//yul las 'das pa'i sgo phye cig/hUM hUM phaT phaT makra ka ru dra sarba te tso phyang //dral ba
90b3(tsha): khog pa dral bar bya//dreg pa ma rungs lha chen khyod//sems kyi sgo kun dang dbye yis//rdo rje 'bar ba'i glu gri yis//gnas kyi sgo kun de phye nas//sems kyi bdag po bzhugs par bya/pra be sha yaT/kho'i gas pa yigs te/de la thabs kyis drangs pa yin//khams gsum
90b4(tsha): nas byung sen rnams//rdo sku'i khyadar gyis//'od zer tshul du bsdus nas ni//de dag bdag gis gzugs la bzung //yi ge de ni tshur 'dus thim//oM prad nye ke sha tsi ta dz+wa la hUM phaT/shes pa gnyis med gyur pa yis//yang dag bsgral la med par bstan//chos nyid skye 'gag
90b5(tsha): med pa la//bde bar gshegs pa phro ba zhes//dbyings su bcug pa gnyi su med//kun kyang mnyam nyid ngo bo nyid//padi snying po byang chub pas/ma skyes pas yang 'gag pa 'jig/bdag nyid ye nas mnyams bu'i thugs rje chen po ji ltar mi mdzad/dbang phyug chen po des/
90b6(tsha): dur khrod brgyad kyis ma mo kun bsdus la//gnas bsrung ba dang las bcol bya//dreg pa chen po'i gzhal yas su//bsgrub pa chen po gsang bsgrub pa//dur khrod brgyad kyi ma mo kun//khrag mthung dam tshig ji bzhin du//byin dang dngos grub 'bebs par gyis//de ltar las thims
90b7(tsha): bstan pa yis/ma dad pa yang dad la btsud//lhag ma med par de bkod do//de ltar gnas rnams bstan//ba'o//rta mchog rol pa'i rgyud las/ma trang chen po gnan pa dang //gnas dang 'bras bu bstan pa'i le'u ste/brgya so drug pa'o///de nas yang ston pa dbang chen
91a1(tsha): kun 'dul gyis//phyag na rdo rje la sogs pa'i 'khor rnams la 'di skad ces bka' stsal to//thad kun tu bzang po'i rang bzhin te/kun ni kun gyi mchog yin no//kun ni kun gyi ngo bo yin//kun zhes mnga' 'brel pa yin//kun zhes nyung la 'dus pa yin du yongs/
91a2(tsha): yang ma chad pa//bzang po bcos med ngang la bya//byang chub sems kyi klong brdal bas//kun tu bzang po rang bzhin la//sangs rgyas med cing sems can med//'khor ba med cing mya ngan med//mtho ris med cing ngan song med//rdzogs khyab rang byung ye shes la//dus gsum sa
91a3(tsha): rgyas dgrar langs kyang /'di la gzung zhing gcad su med//rtog med mnyam nyid chos kyi sku//gzung du med pa gzha' tshon 'dra//gsang ba'i dkyil 'khor zhes bya ba//rdzogs pa chen po'i man ngag gis//byang chub sems las ma yengs na//dkyil 'khor thams cad 'di la

92b1(tsha): ba'i glu dbyangs du ma stong//dkyil 'khol lha la mchod par 'bul//bde mchog dkyil 'khor yangs byed ma/rdo rje reg bya lha mo ste//'dod yon phyag na bsnams pa ni/rkang pa stabs byed lag gar bsgyur//snyan pa'i glu dbyangs blangs nas ni//dkyil 'khor lha la

96a1(tsha): rgyab ba su nakun labyams la gzugs sa gnas pa ye shemasams pas te/mtshon gang da 'apa ngag ste shes kun cis bano/sangs rgyas mang po 'as pa rgya tshogs yang /rgyal bgran gyis gnas chen bskor ba'i gni sasapa/
96a2(tsha): ba'i yi ge 'bras bu bskor bas/dam tshig dbang dang dngos grub 'bebs par byed//gsal 'bar 'od 'bar mtshon gang sku//'phro 'du'i ting 'dzin dgongs pa gsal bar byed//gsang ba'i 'od kyi 'phro 'du kun tu brgyas//bde gshegs rgyal ba kun gyi thugs dam bskul//
96a3(tsha): ma dag sgribs pa 'od kyis sbyong bar byed//tshe dang ye shes dbang dang dngos grub 'dren//bde gshegs dgongs pa ma lus kun gsal nas//thams cad bla med byang chub sa la dgod//'phro 'du gti mug dkar po khu 'phang 'dril//thugs rje byin brlabs 'gro la gnas//de don
96a4(tsha): mchod cing yang dag dngos grub 'bebs//'phro 'du phrin las sgrib byang tshogs bskyed pa//rgyal ba sems dpa' sku la phyag 'tshal bstod//ces bstod//rta mchog rol pa'i rgyud las//ye shes sems dpa' sku la bstod pa'i le'u ste/brgyan drug pa'o//
96a5(tsha): hUM ma tang chen po bsgral ba'i dus/zhi bas phan par ma gyur na//khro bos de bzhin gshegs kun mdzad//khro bo khrag mthung rol pa'i tshogs//'jigs byed 'bar ba'i drug shul can//yes khro bo mi zad pa/mthing nag 'bar ba'i sku lus la//rab pa mthing nag
96a6(tsha): gyen du 'khyil/phyag na gdug pa'i mtshon cha dang //rdo rje gdang chen rang brtags 'dzin/dur khrod chas brgyad sku la brgyan du gsol ka khro mo 'bar ba'i tshogs dang 'khril//tsham rngam drag po'i sgra sgrogs shing //gdug pa ma lus kun 'dul ba//khro mchog
96a7(tsha): 'bar ba khyekhyod la bstod ka/skug nag gdengs pa'i dkyil 'khor nas//khro mo smug nag rab po rdzings//dpal gyi chos brgyad rab tu brgyan//'bar ba'i phyag mtshan mang po 'dzin//khro mo yum mchog mo dang gnyisu med//smug nag brjid pa'i lce la phyag 'tshal
96b1(tsha): bsto dang /ser nag gdengs pa'i dkyil 'khor nas/smug mchog ser nag ma dpung 'bar//phyag mtshan mang po bskal pa'i me dpung 'bar//khro mo yum dang bcas la bstod/de mar nag 'bar ba'i dkyer nas//khro mo dmar nag ral pa bsgyings/dpal gyi chas brgyad
96b2(tsha): brgyan du byas//phyag mtshan mang po rab tu bsnams//khro mo rngam pas rab 'jigs pa//pad+ma'i rgyal po yum dang bcas pa khyod la bstod/ljang nag 'bar ba'i dkyil 'khor nas/khro mchog karma he ru ka/ljang nag zhal gsum phyag drug pa//mi zad khro bo'i
96b3(tsha): chas dang ldan//gdug pa'i phyag mtshan mang po bsnams//phrin las thad grub byed pa//khro mo yum dang bcas la bstod/hUM khro mo khro mo'i tshul du bgrad//khro mo chen mo nyid du gsal//dur khrod rdzas la longs spyod cing /khrag mthung mi zad ral ba can//dpal gyis dgyes

179b1(tsha): chos kyi dbyings kyi dkyil 'khor nas//lhun gyis grub pa'i grong nas//snying po byang chub dgongs pa las//thugs rje chen pos skur ston pa//snang ba mtha' yas tshe dpag med//sprul klu'i byang sems spyan ras gzigs//'dul phyir sku khros rta mgrin dpal//bug+sa/

228a1(tsha): @phyogs te/k+ta lnga dang chas snyam pa//steng nas las kyis rgyas btab nas/rdzogs nas ting 'dzin cha su gsal/mtshams nas bsrung dang go cha 'o//phyag rgya skye dang go cha dang /drod chang spang dang thugs rje dbab/tshe gnas gtim dang lnga pa'o//yang nga
228a2(tsha): /yang du bsnyen par bya/'phrin las skabs dang sbyar nas bcom/dbang dang dam tshig mi bza' ste/zhi ba ni la dkyil 'khor gyi/'phro 'du rim gyis bsnyen par bya//bskul dang dor byas 'das pa dang /cho ga gsum gyis snang mdzad pa//semasu zhu nas dbyingasu thi ma/skye med
228a3(tsha): dbyi su dngos med sku//hUM du gyur nas rtse lnga 'bar//bskul nas don byas mi skyod sku//sem su zhu nas dbyingasu thim//skye med dbyings nas dngos med sku//swa las rin chen zur brgyad 'phro//bskul dang don byas 'byung ldan nyid//semasu zhu nas dbyings nyid//skye med byangs rba
228a4(tsha): dngos med sku/au las pad mor gsal ba las/bskul nas don byas snang mtha' yas/sem su zhu nas dbyings nyid//skye med dbyings nas dngos med sku'o//ha las rgya gram 'bar 'phro ba/bskul nas don byas don grub pa'o//sem su zhu nas dbyings nyid//'phro 'dugs
228a5(tsha): dadapa la su rung //dngosu grub nas mya ngan 'das//mang du sbrel phyir bsnyen par sbyar/grub pas dngos grub gsol ba dang /bstan par bya phyir bdud rtsi gnod/yum gyis gsang ba'i tshig du'o//sngags dang ldan pa dbang bskur te/gnas lnga dag tu thim par bya//de nas rgya tsam mdud pa

228a7(tsha): kyang rje bkra shis tshig tu bcad pa bgrags//rang bzhin dkyil 'khor rdzogs pa dang /gzugs bsnyen bud d+ha nyid la 'bul/tsit+ta la sogs rang bzhin rdzas/rang bzhin mchod pa nyid du'o//rna ba sgra'i mchod pa yis//glog 'gyur nyid dbyingasu bstim//tri ni a mo de bas ste/spros pa/

228b4(tsha): dbyings dang ldan pa'i ye shes dang /sgyu ma lho nub mtsham su ste/de bzhin khams brgyad nyid la mchod//kham pa ye shes kham brgya la'o//dbyangs dang tshig ldan snga ma'i ngang //skal tshig phyogs bzhi gru chad la'o//lang ka'i tshogs la dbul ba dang /dbyangs dang ldan pa'i tshig dag

228b6(tsha): ma'i tshul//me ri las ni ba su ta/skyung mo brgyad la mchod pa yang //ma skyes dbyings nas thim par bya//bsu ba'i thabs ni 'di dag gsungs//dbyings kyi cho 'phrul rab smros te/bskor ma byed ye shes bya//spyang mo dgu dgu brtsegs pa la//dbu dgu nyid kyang spyan long ste/dbu
228b7(tsha): long long pa'i tshogs la 'phros//long ba khams rgya chen po la//rang nyid gtso ba yab yum la'o//sum yang dpal be'u'i thugs la bsdu//dpal le'u skye med dbyingasu ste/brjod pa med pa'i tshul du thim/bdag dang dkyil 'khor ji bzhin te/bdag nyid ma btang nyid las

229a6(tsha): ldan gsang ba'i yum nyi //rigs la dbang rnams mi bza' bar//thad yong su rdzogs pa'i phyir//khrag 'thung lnga po'i dbu la rgyud//khrag 'thung chen po'i ngabu rgyud/khro bo chen po'i mthu chen brgyad/bsgral nas zhabs kyi gdan la rgyud/rdzogs pa chen po yin pa'i phyir/ting 'dzin brgyad dang ldan

229b4(tsha): pa yang //smra ba med pa'i gsung du rig/rig pa'i don la 'angar mi bya//bsam du med pa'i sems gang yin//de ni thugs kyi rang bzhin te//de las 'da' ba ma yino//sku gsung thugs las ma g.yos kyang //che ba'i yon tan sna tshogs 'byung //'on kyang zad med 'phel med pas/
229b5(tsha): yon tan dam tshig yin par grags//de las 'da' bar yong mi bya//brtsal ba med las gang yin pa//de ni thabs kyi spyod pa bas//phrin las dam tshig 'da' ka ba'o//lnga po 'di la gang gnas pa//rnal 'byor ska ldan dam pa'o//bsgrub par bya ba'i dam tshig chen po ni//byang chub sems dang sha

230a1(tsha): @#dam tshig ni//pad+ma can dang pad can ma//ban glang ma dang ri mo can//bu mo mig rigs la sogs kun//spyad par gyur pas sbyar ba yang //mthong ba dam tshig yin pas te/dbang po'i yul dang shes mtshon ldan//bsgrub par bya ba'i dam tshig ni//nyon mongs lnga

233b4(tsha): srog bdag gsang ba'i dbusu sbyangs//ljags kyi dbang po 'khyil ba dang //na sa'i dbyings nas glad par bskul//tshor ba nyam su myong nas ni ka/rgal ba'i rtsa ru brgyud nas kyang //gsang ba'i rdo rje lam du byangs//ba ga gsang ba'i dkyil 'khor du//yang dang yang du spro zhing bsdu//nyi ma zla ba pad+ma/

234b1(tsha): /mgan skyabs rgyun du mdzad pas brjid kyang che//dgos pa'i don rnam grub pas brjid kyang che//de ltar dpal chen po yon tan sum cu rtsa drug gis/dug gsum sku gsung thug su sbyar//sdug bsngal bde ba chen por bcad//las ni ye shes chen po ste/gang 'gro mtho ris rgyas btab pas/

241b1(tsha): gzhung bzhin spe la//phrin las bcol ba snga ma bzhin ka ya ba yum gnyis med sbyor ba las//sems kyi kha dog dkar po'i yi ge ste//hri mtha' mnyam spros shing slar yang bsdu//dngos po med cing stong par gyur par dmigs//lha mo mgo dgu ma dang rol pa las//byang chub sems ni gu ya dman//
241b2(tsha): bo spro//rus pa mtshan med ra ga bzhin//dra ba mo dang gnyis med rol pa las//byang chub sems ni hUM gi stong khams gang //bskul bzhin dus byas 'bar ba 'phros par dmigs ka ka/a mo de bas gnyis med gnyis gyur nas//sems ni kha dog dkar dmar snol ma yang /
241b3(tsha): skar tshogs lta bur rab tu 'khrigs pas bskul//bsdus pas tshim gyur gtso ma'i kha dog bzhin//sder mo can gyis ltar byang chub sems//rin chen gzhan ma lta bur bskul sbyangs nas//dngos min byang chub semasu zhu//de nas dngos grub blang ba'i phyir//sgrub pa 'de ba'i/
241b4(tsha): mtsham ldan yo byad bas//rag pu rtsa/zhes brjod cing /bral chen 'odu zhu ba rang migs//tam las rlung gis brteg nas kyang /gzi ldan gsang ba b+ha ga las rang bzhin mda'i tshe skur gsal//mthar phyin snyems shing mgo rgyud sbyangs//slar yang mi g.yo dbyingasu thim//de nas
241b5(tsha): gsang ba'i mdud pa dgrol//snum la 'phel 'gyur grab pa'i rtags//nyung na spogs shing ting 'dzin las//grub nas ngo la mtshan mas gdam//mtshan ldan lag mthil a bsams te/bros bkor dbang bskur byin gyis brlab//de nas dngos grub go skabs dbye//
241b6(tsha): sa ma ya pad+ma da ra bu tsa me/dang po de bi gdab par bya//de nas tshe dang rim pa bzhin//chad na bar chad chen po 'byung //lhag na rim pa bzhin du 'gro//rdo rje slob dpon yab yum dang //mched dang dgongs pa'i tshad dang sbyar//de nas lus la btags bcangs nas/sku
241b7(tsha): gsung zugs kyis dngos grub thob//lus kyang ngar la bab cing sra/longs spyod ldan zhing ba skying 'gyur//de nas lhag ma'i gtor ma btang //dkyil 'khor bsdu zhing bkra gis gsol//yal bar mi dang ma mo bden//klu dang dbyangs dang rnga la sogs//rtse 'jom sa
243a1(tsha): @#rab bsgoms nas//'bras bu pha rol byin drug rang bzhin rdzogs//rang sems na mkha ma bkag dang la banag/'on gtang drang ba ni rtog pa ci byung yang //chos nyid ma g.yos rtog par ma gcad pas//nga gis ma bcos yongs kyis 'od gsal ces//snga rams mkhas/
243a2(tsha): ldan rnams kyis rab tu bsngags//yid dang sems la gsal byed ting 'dzin te//yo ga gsum don gnas skabs den gsal//don de bas na stan khung rab rgyud nas//sems nyid sangs rgyas don du shes par bya'o//de lta bu'i don rtogs gang rab skyes bu la//ci yang mi rtse yon gyi rnam grangs 'bul//
243a3(tsha): 'on tang byas zin don rtogs na/ye nas bdag nyid rna raM dag ces byas//de la zin tshags bde sdug ci byung /tshogs gnyis dus cig rdzogs par dam bcas pa'o//rnam par mi rtog ting 'dzin mtshan ma rnam dag pa/chos nyid mngon sum gyur na che yon rgyun mi chad/sgrib pa gnyis sbyangs tshogs gnyis rdzogs
243a4(tsha): bas na//phyi nang bzhed dang mthun par sa rnams gnon//'di ni bde gshegs dgongs spyod zab mo yin//sems can phal gyi spyod yul ma yino//sangs rgyas rnams kyis mnyam pa'i don rtogs kyang //'gro ba rnams kyis ma rtogs pas//sdod nas zhen pa'i bag chags sbyangs dka' ba//sa dang sa'i
243a5(tsha): ngas khyad yod gsungs//mnyam par rtogs dang ma rtogs gnyis med gsal du su//sangs rgyas che ba'i yon tan lhan gyis grub mnyes//stong gdang rang bzhin med cing ma skyes khyad par cika/yod med rtag chad bya rtsal la sogs gnyis po dag/rtogs dang ma rtogs re rtsol gnas pa'i
243a6(tsha): skyon tsam gyis//rgyu 'bras so sor gsal bar smra na yang //lha skyong mnyam pa'i don de shes bya dgos//lha spyod ye nas 'du 'bral med bzhin du//mi mnyam pa'i spyod yul cir snang yang //phyag rgya'i dkyil 'khor gang yang med//gnas skabs de mtshogs rdzogs thabs dang shes rab
243a7(tsha): 'brel du den mi nyid na dgongs pa chos thams cad//don du ye gas chen po'i semasu 'dus//sems kyis ye shes rang gis ma bcos pas//ting 'dzin gyi dmigs pa thad bral//de lta bu'i byang chub bde che ni ka/bgrod pa'i sa dang lta ba'i gzugs med pas//mtshan mar brtags spyo/
243b1(tsha): ba lus mkhyen/theg rgyal yang rje the tshom gags bsal zhing /bla med dngos grub chen po la zla'o//de bas dbu ma'i lta ba cha 'dra ba//thams cad ma lus dbang du rab bsdus te//gzhan las spangs pa'i don du 'di gsung so//mdo sde la sogs phyi nang yul dag la//dag pa'i mtshan ma tsam du/
243b2(tsha): gnas pa dang /rnal 'byor spyod pa'i tshig rnams semasu 'dus//mtha' gnyis dang gis gsal zhes smra ba yang /mnyam par rtog pas gud na chos med do//gzhan na yod par 'dod na bag chags skyon yin//de bas dgongs chos so sor sgra snang yang //byas pa'i spyod yul 'khrul snang
243b3(tsha): nyid snang yang /sangs rgyas sems can sgyu ma mig rgyu'i tshul/ye shes skye 'gag med pa na mkha' bzhin//de'i dus na 'das de mthong ba med//de yang mi snang nyid na skyon med na//'khrul pa yin pas bsdus pa ma yino//mthong dang ma mthong snang ba rang bzhin med//dgod sbyong
243b4(tsha): 'i man ngag thaMd ni//rang rig ye shes mngon du gsal dus na//dag pa'i ye shes sangs rgyas mthong ces bya//de ltar ye shes sangs rgyas mthong ba'i tshe//bag chagase med cing yul las 'das pa'i tshe//bya med snang med mi brtsal bzhag pa yin//dmigs pa'i yul med chos la
243b5(tsha): ga chags pas//mthong tshor bde ba'i lam med mu med gsal ba dbyings//'gro zlog skye gtsug ye shes dbyings kyi g.yo//de yang tha tshig mdo sde zab mo'i nang //de lha'i don la sangs rgyas mdzad mi mdzad//chos rnams thaMd rig pa'i ye shes sna de//de 'brel yan chad gdod
243b6(tsha): nas ma skyes te/rgyu dang 'bras bu rtag pas yong ye med//'di ltar rig na chos nyid lhun gyis grub//don la mnyam par gzhag pa'i skyon chen ni//lung dang 'gal cing don dang 'phrad mi 'gyur//don la des blos khyab par btang nas ni ka/rang bzhin ye shes dag pa ste//rigs gsal dang na thad
243b7(tsha): gnyi su med//byang chub snying po rgyu 'bras lhan gru bo//nga'i drung na drod gsum man ngag rdzogs//snang dang mi snang thad la sogs pa//shes rab zab mo'i nges pa'i ting 'dzin de//phyi nang lta ba'i theg pa'i thig gang po//'di'i don la gsang thub gasran tshugs shing //tshig dang don du 'brel pas thag
